### [66] [Is Solving Better Than Evaluating GenAI Solutions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27586)
*Ethan Dickey,Marios Mertzanidis,Alexandros Psomas*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 在一门大三算法课程中开展了一项随机A/B交叉实验（N=220），比较评估AI生成解决方案与传统问题求解两种教学模式，发现两组在中考、期末考试、总成绩及结构化匹配的试题上均无显著差异；评估AI方案的学生作业得分更高，但未转化为后续总结性学习收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI能够越来越熟练地解决编程作业，计算教育界开始探索强调方案评估、验证和批判的教学方法。然而，关于以评估为中心的任务对学生学习影响的证据仍然有限，特别是在高年级、理论密集的课程中。

Method: 在一门大三算法课程中进行了随机A/B交叉实验（N=220）。跨六个作业，学生工作组要么直接解决有挑战性的算法问题，要么评估经常存在缺陷的AI生成解决方案；学期中途角色互换。通过对比中考试、期末考试成绩、总成绩以及结构对齐的考试题分析学习效果，并结合调查数据了解学生学习习惯变化。

Result: 两组在中考试、期末考试成绩、总成绩以及与作业干预结构对齐的考试题上均无统计显著差异。评估AI生成方案的学生获得显著更高的作业分数，但这种局部优势并未转化为下游总结性学习收益。调查显示多数学生报告未改变学习习惯，但调整学习策略的学生认为AI评估作业显著更有帮助。

Conclusion: AI生成方案评估将学生的努力从开放式方案构建重新分配至验证、诊断和判断，但不会自动产生更强的概念迁移。AI评估活动可以纳入算法课程而不造成广泛性能损失，但有意义的学习收益可能需要精心设计的脚手架来推动学生超越简单的错误诊断。

Abstract: As Generative AI (GenAI) tools become increasingly capable of generating solutions to computing assignments, the computing education community is exploring pedagogical approaches that emphasize solution evaluation, verification, and critique alongside traditional solution generation. However, evidence regarding the impact of such evaluation-centered tasks on student learning remains limited, particularly in upper-division, theory-heavy courses. We conducted a randomized A/B crossover study (N=220) in a junior-level algorithms course to compare evaluating GenAI-generated solutions with traditional problem solving. Across six assignments, student working groups either solved challenging algorithmic problems directly or evaluated often-flawed GenAI-generated solutions, with roles reversed midway through the semester.
  We found no statistically significant differences between groups in midterm scores, final exam scores, overall course grades, or exam problems structurally aligned with the homework interventions. Students received significantly higher homework scores when evaluating GenAI-generated solutions, but this localized advantage did not translate into downstream summative gains. Survey data further indicated that most students reported no change in study habits in response to the intervention; however, those who reported adapting their study strategies rated the GenAI-evaluation assignments as significantly more helpful. These findings suggest that GenAI evaluation redistributes student effort from open-ended solution construction toward verification, diagnosis, and judgment, but does not automatically produce stronger conceptual transfer. We conclude that GenAI-evaluation activities can be incorporated into algorithms coursework without broad performance losses, but meaningful learning gains may require deliberate scaffolding that pushes students beyond simple error diagnosis.

</details>


### [67] [Scaling, Lock-In, and Proxy Compliance: A Political Economy of Responsible AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28023)
*Florian A. D. Burnat,Brittany I. Davidson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个顺序政治经济学模型，分析AI供应商、部署者和监管者三方互动下的问责机制。研究发现，供应商可能停留在"可观察的采购底线"（proxy-compliance equilibrium），即形式上合规但缓解措施低于社会最优水平。文章刻画了唯一内部均衡及完全缓解的角点解，并探讨了独立审计权、可移植性、事件报告和结果关联责任四种机制的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI问责面临规模化难题，其本质是制度问题——谁来观察、验证和改变已部署系统。现有研究缺乏对部署者监测行为与供应商决策之间战略互动的系统分析，特别是切换成本如何影响监测激励、以及形式合规与实际运营成果之间的差距。

Method: 构建顺序政治经济学模型：供应商先选择可审计性和实质性缓解措施；部署者采用后面临切换成本并进行监测；执法取决于可验证证据。通过均衡分析刻画供应商预期部署者监测反应下的最优决策，区分内部均衡和角点解，并比较不同制度工具的效应。

Result: 存在"代理合规均衡"：供应商可停留在可观察的采购底线，缓解水平低于社会最优。独立审计权直接提高执法暴露度；可移植性恢复部署者的议价杠杆；事件报告增加监管者可验证的证据渠道；结果关联责任创造不依赖供应商控制检测的激励。模型解释了为什么文档记录和标准化评估能与持续部署后伤害共存。

Conclusion: AI问责的制度设计需要超越单一工具。该研究为监测、缓解及形式合规与运营成果之间的差距提供了可检验的预测，表明仅靠文档化和标准化评估不足以保障实质性的安全缓解。

Abstract: AI accountability at scale is an institutional problem: who can observe, verify, and change deployed systems. We develop a sequential political-economy model in which an AI vendor chooses auditability and substantive mitigation, a deployer monitors after adoption while facing switching costs, and enforcement depends on verifiable evidence. Anticipating the deployer's monitoring response, the vendor may stop at an observable procurement floor while mitigating below the social first best, producing a proxy-compliance equilibrium. We characterize the unique interior equilibrium and the corner in which harm is fully mitigated. Independent audit rights raise enforcement exposure directly; portability restores deployer leverage; incident reporting adds a regulator-visible evidence channel; and outcome-linked liability creates incentives that do not depend on vendor-controlled detection. The results explain why documentation and standardized evaluations can coexist with persistent post-deployment harms, and generate testable implications for monitoring, mitigation, and the gap between formal compliance and operational outcomes.

</details>


### [68] [When AI Does the Work, What Is Learning For? Post-Instrumental Learning and the Risk of Capacity Dissolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28041)
*Kai Yao*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"后工具性学习"（post-instrumental learning）概念，主张即使AI完美执行任务，教育仍须培养人们设定目的、给出理由、质疑、拒绝/修订和参与的五种核心能力，并以生成式AI下的评估为例说明机构应评估学习者与AI协作中的责任关系，而非仅看成品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI伦理仅聚焦于当前缺陷（偏见、幻觉、隐私等），导致一种逻辑困境：若学习的合法性仅依赖AI的失败，那么AI技术每进步一次，教育的理由就弱一分。作者意图超越这种"失败驱动"的论证，为AI时代的教与学提供更稳健的辩护。

Method: 采用理想化方法：设想一种"完美AI"——能无缺陷地执行指定任务，但对目的、合法性与责任无权威。在此理想化框架下，作者提出"后工具性学习"概念，辨识五种不可委托的能力（end-setting, reason-giving, contestability, refusal/revision, participation），并将其侵蚀命名为"能力消解"（capacity dissolution）。以生成式AI下的评估作为核心案例进行分析。

Result: 提出了一套超越AI缺陷的规范性框架：当AI生成的精美成品不再能可靠地证明理解时，机构应转向评估学习者与AI中介工作之间的问责关系，而非成品本身。文章还给出实践启示：AI治理应考察系统部署是否损害人们理解、挑战、修订和分担责任的能力。

Conclusion: 学习的价值不应绑定于AI的失败；即使在AI完美执行的情况下，保持人类的五项核心能力仍是教育和治理的根本目标。AI治理的评价标准应从"系统表现好坏"扩展到"系统部署是否侵蚀人类能力"。

Abstract: As AI systems become capable of producing the essays, code, reports, summaries, plans, and decisions through which institutions usually recognize competence, a familiar question becomes harder to answer: what is learning for? Existing AI ethics rightly emphasizes present failures--bias, opacity, hallucination, labor extraction, privacy risk, and weak accountability. But if the case for learning rests only on those failures, then each technical improvement appears to weaken it. This article develops a different answer. Using the idealization of AI that executes specified tasks flawlessly while lacking authority over purposes, legitimacy, and responsibility, we argue for post-instrumental learning: learning that preserves the capacities people and institutions need when many useful outputs can be delegated. We analyze five such capacities--end-setting, reason-giving, contestability, refusal/revision, and participation--and name their erosion capacity dissolution. The central case is assessment under generative AI. When a polished artifact no longer reliably evidences understanding, institutions must assess the learner's accountable relation to AI-mediated work rather than the artifact alone. The takeaway is practical: AI governance should evaluate not only whether systems perform well, but also whether their deployment leaves people able to understand, challenge, revise, and share responsibility for the practices those systems mediate.

</details>


### [69] [Asymmetric Communication: Large Language Models and Language Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28137)
*Enzo Fenoglio*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文论证当代AI话语将人类交际实践中构成的性质（通用智能、幻觉、能动性、感受性、对齐）错误地投射到语言模型上，提出"不对称通信"框架来阐释这一范畴错误的本质——模型输出虽参与交际循环，却不承担规范承诺、资格或评估。该不对称性由三个结构性条件定义，不依赖于模型能力，并重新将"对齐"定性为制度约束工程而非智能体间的目标同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代AI讨论中存在系统性误归因：人们赋予语言模型它们无法承载的性质，如通用智能、认知失败的幻觉、自主能动性、涌现感受性和对齐目标同步。作者认为这些是同一类范畴错误的实例，亟需一个统一的解释框架来阐明其结构，并由此得出AI治理的理论基础。

Method: 本文提出"不对称通信"（asymmetric communication）框架，定义三个结构性条件：(i) 正确性仅由接收者执行；(ii) 责任仅由人类参与者承担；(iii) 输出的实际地位完全取决于人类的采纳。框架综合了维特根斯坦（意义在共享实践中产生）、卢曼（沟通在接收者侧完成）、埃斯波西托（算法偶然性足以引发采纳）和布兰顿（规范记分是话语地位的来源）四个理论资源，并将此框架应用于五种误归因现象。

Result: 该框架成功将全部五种现象（通用智能、幻觉、能动性、感受性、对齐）重新分类为接收者侧现象：幻觉被重构为人类评估问题而非模型认知失败；对齐被重新定义为制度约束工程而非智能体间目标同步。这三个条件是结构性的，独立于能力，即便更强大的模型提高了误归因的风险，不对称性依然不变。由此护栏（guardrails）被论证为结构性必然而非机器道德能动性的表现，责任仍由人类制度承担。

Conclusion: 核心结论是：人类与LLM的交互构成一种"语言游戏"，其中只有一方承载全部规范活动。模型输出参与交际循环但不承担承诺、行使权利或执行评估，因此不具备话语地位。对齐本质上是人类制度的约束工程，AI治理的责任归属保持在人道机构一方。这为AI话语提供了概念上的纠偏，并给出治理上的结构性理由。

Abstract: Contemporary AI discourse attributes to language models properties they cannot bear: general intelligence as substrate-independent cognition, hallucination as cognitive failure, agency as autonomous goal-pursuit, sentience as emergent inner life, alignment as goal synchronization. This paper argues that these are instances of a single category mistake--properties constituted within human communicative practice are projected onto the machine side--and explains its structure. Human-LLM interaction constitutes a language game in which one side bears all normative activity. We call this configuration asymmetric communication since model outputs circulate communicatively, entering further exchanges, without the system undertaking commitments, bearing entitlements, or performing the assessment on which discursive standing depends. Three conditions define the asymmetry: (i) correctness is enforced exclusively by the receiver; (ii) accountability is borne by human participants alone; and (iii) the practical standing of any output depends entirely on human uptake. These conditions are structural, hold independently of capability, and remain unchanged as more powerful models raise the stakes of misattribution. The framework draws on Wittgenstein (meaning enacted in shared practices), Luhmann (communication completed on the receiver's side), Esposito (algorithmic contingency sufficient for uptake), and Brandom (normative scorekeeping as the source of discursive standing). Applied to all five, it reclassifies each as a receiver-side phenomenon, grounds guardrails as structural necessities rather than manifestations of machine moral agency, and yields an implication for AI governance. Alignment is institutional constraint engineering, not goal synchronization between agents, while responsibility remains with human institutions.

</details>


### [70] [Technology-Enhanced Tabletop Exercises for Cybersecurity Education: Lessons Learned](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28179)
*Jan Vykopal,Pavel Čeleda,Martin Horák,Valdemar Švábenský*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将技术增强的桌面推演（TTXs）整合到计算机教育尤其是网络安全课程中的实践。作者通过INJECT Exercise Platform (IXP)这一基于Web的平台自动化场景流程并实现数据驱动的评估，从2024到2026年运行了25次演练、涵盖743名参与者，分享了24条经验教训，证明数字化TTX为网络安全课程及其他需要团队解决问题的课程提供了可扩展、可复制的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的桌面推演（TTXs）在专业实践中已得到广泛应用，但在大学教育中仍未被充分利用。作者希望通过技术手段填补这一空白，更好地培养学生应对事件响应和IT治理中复杂协作问题解决能力（包括协调、沟通和及时决策）。

Method: 作者通过INJECT Exercise Platform (IXP)这一基于Web的环境来增强TTX的交付与评估。IXP自动传递场景更新、促进团队讨论、收集交互数据以支持自动化评估，从而增强真实感、减少教师工作量，并为学生学习提供可操作的洞察。

Result: 从2024年至2026年，作者在多个大学课程和课外活动中运行了25次演练，共有743名参与者。观察到学生参与度和协作性提高，教师对团队如何应对复杂场景有了更清晰的可见性，并总结了24条经验教训。

Conclusion: 本文证明了数字化TTX为网络安全课程及其他需要团队问题解决的课程提供了可扩展且可复制的模型，为教师和课程设计者提供了将技术增强TTX融入教学的具体指导。

Abstract: This innovative practice full paper examines the integration of technology-enhanced tabletop exercises (TTXs) into computing education, focusing on cybersecurity curricula. The motivation is to better prepare students for complex, collaborative problem solving typical of incident response and IT governance, where coordination, communication, and timely decision-making are essential. Although TTXs are well-established in professional practice, they remain underused in universities. We address this gap by augmenting TTX delivery and evaluation through the INJECT Exercise Platform (IXP), a web-based environment that automates scenario flow and enables data-driven assessment. Our practice implements IXP to automatically deliver scenario updates, facilitate team discussions, and collect interaction data to support automated assessment. This combination enhances realism, reduces instructor workload, and provides actionable insight into student learning. From 2024 to 2026, we ran 25 exercises with 743 participants in multiple university courses and extracurricular events. We observed increased engagement and collaboration among students, and clearer visibility for instructors into how teams navigate complex scenarios. This paper shares 24 lessons learned from these exercises. Instructors and curriculum designers may benefit from concrete guidance for integrating technology-enhanced TTXs. We demonstrate that digital TTXs provide a scalable and replicable model for cybersecurity courses and others requiring team-based problem-solving.

</details>


### [71] [When AI Becomes Routine: A Decade of Public AI Mediation in Korean Go Commentary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28332)
*Haewoon Kwak*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了韩国YouTube围棋解说中AI（如KataGo）如何从显式命名转向隐式呈现的现象。基于2016-2025年约1900小时的语料，发现AI胜率图在98%的机构直播画面中出现，但AI相关话语仅占2.63%的句子。作者将这种"AI来源标签隐退"现象解读为AI驯化（domestication）的传播学标志，并提出区分"来源前置"与"来源隐退"两种中介类型的类型学框架，讨论其对抗议性（contestability）的不同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当AI系统超越人类专家水平并融入日常实践后，机器判断如何被公众理解和归因成为一个关键问题。作者希望探究AI在真实专业实践中的"可理解性"（public intelligibility）与"可归责性"（attributability）如何演化，并关注这种演化在AI可靠性较低的领域中带来的风险差异。

Method: 作者收集了韩国YouTube围棋解说语料，覆盖2016-2025年约1900小时视频，包括机构直播和创作者频道，按AI可用性划分为四个阶段。通过定量分析统计AI可视化占比与AI相关话语比例，并通过定性分析发展出"来源前置vs来源隐退"的中介类型学。

Result: 研究发现：晚期机构直播中AI胜率图可见率约98%，但AI相关话语仅占2.63%的句子。胜率和点差讨论持续存在，但"AI"一词本身逐渐不被人提及。创作者主导的解说比机构解说更倾向于以界面渲染取代显式命名。这一"来源标签隐退"现象被解读为AI驯化的传播学特征。

Conclusion: AI在围棋解说中被"驯化"后，机器来源从显式命名转向隐式界面呈现，形成"来源前置"与"来源隐退"两种不同的中介模式，二者保留了不同形式的可议性锚点（discursive anchors）。这一发现的利害关系在AI可靠性低于围棋的领域中会更加显著。

Abstract: When AI systems surpass elite human performance and settle into everyday expert practice, the question that follows is how machine judgment is made publicly intelligible and attributable. We study Korean Go commentary on YouTube, where AI systems such as KataGo became standard analytic tools after AlphaGo. Our corpus spans a decade (2016--2025) and approximately $1{,}900$ hours of footage across institutional broadcasters and creator-led channels, in four phases of AI availability. We document a widening asymmetry between visual and verbal AI presence: AI winrate graphs are visible for about $98\%$ of late-period institutional broadcast time, yet AI-salient talk accounts for only $2.63\%$ of sentences. What recedes is the source label, not the metric: winrate and point-gap talk persists while ``AI'' itself goes unsaid. We read this recession as the communicative signature of domestication. Our strongest evidence is a compositional shift in verbal mediation: explicit naming gives way to interface rendering, and creator-led commentary leans further toward it than institutional commentary. We develop a typology distinguishing source-foregrounding from source-receding mediation, and argue that the two preserve different hooks of contestability: discursive anchors through which audiences can recognize and question the machine source. The stakes of that difference rise in domains where AI is less reliable than in Go.

</details>


### [72] [AIx4Soccer: A Unified Platform Architecture for Football Club Management and Structured Athlete Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28531)
*Frederico Falconi Costa,Salvador Cesar Costa,Fabricio F. Costa*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文从概念层面提出了"AIx4Soccer One Platform"——一个多租户云SaaS架构，旨在统一足球俱乐部的碎片化数字工具（视频分析、GPS追踪、医疗记录、球探和管理系统），特别针对巴西等发展中市场草根俱乐部面临的数字鸿沟问题。平台核心包括PDI框架（球员个人发展计划）、Tak Tik双边市场以及事件驱动的语义数据模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足球俱乐部运营着大量碎片化、互不兼容的数字工具，这种碎片化在非精英俱乐部（尤其是巴西等发展中市场的草根俱乐部）中最为严重，造成了数字鸿沟。尽管巴西是全球最大的职业球员出口国，但其草根俱乐部却无法负担集成系统所需的成本，亟需一个统一、经济可行的一体化解决方案。

Method: 论文提出了一个多租户云SaaS平台的架构设计：(1)统一俱乐部管理流程的"AIx4Soccer One Platform"；(2)PDI框架作为结构化的球员发展方法论，将发展计划与视频证据和周期性审查关联；(3)Tak Tik双边市场连接俱乐部与视频分析师，采用75%/25%的收入分成模式；(4)事件驱动语义数据模型作为底层技术基座，每个事实都是类型化的不可变事件，追加到日志中并构建知识图谱；建议使用小型领域特定模型而非前沿大语言模型作为智能层。

Result: 本文是一篇设计性和早期部署论文，而非实证评估。作者提供了正式、实现无关的平台规格说明（事件驱动语义数据模型），阐述了系统需求，并针对运动员发展框架、体育分析工作流、双边市场经济学和多租户SaaS模式进行了文献定位。同时讨论了青年数据保护义务（巴西LGPD和2025数字ECA、欧盟GDPR）、人才评估中的算法公平性风险。论文明确不作任何功效声明。

Conclusion: 该研究为发展中市场草根足球俱乐部提供了一种概念性的一体化数字平台架构方案，通过SaaS模式、双边市场和事件驱动数据模型来弥合数字鸿沟，同时正视了数据隐私、算法公平性和技术选型（小模型优先）等关键问题。后续需要实证评估来验证其有效性。

Abstract: Football clubs, academies, and federations operate a growing but fragmented portfolio of digital tools: separate systems for video analysis, GPS/performance tracking, medical records, scouting, and administration. This fragmentation is most acute outside the elite European clubs that can afford integration, producing a digital divide that disadvantages grassroots clubs in developing markets such as Brazil, paradoxically the world's largest exporter of professional players. This paper presents, at a conceptual level, the architecture of "AIx4Soccer One Platform," a multi-tenant cloud SaaS operating system that unifies club-management workflows and embeds a structured athlete-development methodology, the PDI Framework (Plano de Desenvolvimento Individual / Individual Development Plan). We describe two companion components: "Tak Tik," a certified two-sided marketplace connecting clubs with video analysts under a 75%/25% (analyst/platform) revenue split, and the PDI/TBIL methodology, which links development plans to video evidence and periodic review. As Materials and Methods, we state explicit requirements and give a formal, implementation-independent specification of the platform's proposed future substrate: an event-centric semantic data model in which every fact is a typed, immutable event in an append-only log that induces a growing knowledge graph. We situate the design against the literature on athlete-development frameworks, sports-analytics workflows, two-sided-market economics, and multi-tenant SaaS patterns, and discuss youth data-protection obligations (Brazil's LGPD and 2025 Digital ECA; the EU GDPR), algorithmic-fairness risks in talent evaluation, and why small, domain-specific models, rather than frontier LLMs, are the appropriate intelligence layer. This is a design and early-deployment paper, not an empirical evaluation, making no efficacy claims.

</details>


### [73] [Correcting Mode Collapse in Silicon Sampling with Semantic Similarity Rating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28550)
*Oscar Heath,Rohan Alexander*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了硅采样（使用LLM生成调查回复）中回复分布方差过低的问题，提出使用语义相似度评级（Semantic Similarity Rating）方法，即让LLM生成纯文本回复，再通过文本嵌入映射到数值量表，以提高政治态度调查中硅采样回复的保真度。实验表明该方法既改善了回复分布的保真度，又只需少量校准参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅采样（使用LLM生成调查响应）虽有前景，但生成的响应分布往往方差过低（模态坍缩），作者认为这是因为LLM在生成数值数据方面存在缺陷，因此需要探索使用文本响应替代数值响应来提升采样保真度。

Method: 采用语义相似度评级方法：让LLM仅生成纯文本回复（不作数值输出），然后使用文本嵌入将这些文本回复映射到数值量表上，实现对政治态度调查的硅采样。

Result: 实验发现，语义相似度评级方法能够提升硅采样响应分布的保真度（减少模态坍缩问题），同时该方法所需校准的参数数量很少。

Conclusion: 文本响应配合语义相似度映射是提升硅采样保真度的有效途径，为LLM生成调查数据提供了更可靠的方法，并且具有参数友好、易于部署的优势。

Abstract: Silicon sampling refers to the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate responses to surveys. It has shown promise, but tends to generate response distributions with unrealistically low variance. We argue that this mode collapse is due to LLMs failure to generate numeric data, and that text responses may be better suited for this task. We analyze whether Semantic Similarity Rating can improve the fidelity of silicon sampling responses when asked about political attitudes. This method solicits text-only responses from LLMs, then maps this to a numeric scale using text embeddings. We find that this method both improves the fidelity of silicon sampling response distributions, and has few parameters to calibrate.

</details>

### [74] [Are Three Matrices All You Need To Beat the Market? Observable Matrix Dynamics for Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27461)
*Igor Halperin*

Main category: q-fin.PM

TL;DR: 本文提出一个仅基于每日价格、成交量与市值的动态投资组合管理框架，使用三个固定大小的矩阵（收益相关距离矩阵及两个基于滚动收益和滚动波动率的马尔可夫链转移矩阵）替代Markowitz的期望收益向量与协方差矩阵。该方法无需矩阵求逆，基于稳健的横截面排名，且是动态的。实证表明波动率排名可预测而收益排名接近不可预测，基于预测构建的市场中性动量多空策略叠加机会型纯多头，在两个样本外测试期均跑赢市场（Sharpe 1.06/1.32 vs 0.78/1.14），并优于经典最小方差与最大分散化组合。通过残差距离分散多头部分进一步提升表现，凸信息领导者叠加层则保护市场中性部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Markowitz均值-方差优化依赖期望收益向量与协方差矩阵，存在矩阵求逆、对异常值敏感、单期静态等局限。作者希望构建一个更简单、稳健且动态的组合管理框架，仅利用公开可得的市场数据。

Method: 框架状态由三个固定大小矩阵构成：(1)收益相关性的距离矩阵；(2)基于月度滚动收益排名的马尔可夫链转移矩阵；(3)基于月度滚动波动率排名的马尔可夫链转移矩阵。方法无需矩阵求逆，使用异常值稳健的横截面排名，并以动态方式运作。组合构建采用市场中性动量多空策略叠加机会型纯多头，并用残差距离分散多头部分，另以凸信息领导者叠加层为市场中性部分提供保险。

Result: 在2022年1月至2024年12月和2025年1月至2026年7月两个非重叠样本外测试期，组合Sharpe分别为1.06和1.32，优于市场的0.78和1.14（净交易成本5个基点、每日盯市）。优于经典最小方差和最大分散化组合。用残差距离分散多头部分后，Sharpe提升至1.08和1.44，年化收益从18%提升至20%、从44%提升至56%。凸信息领导者叠加层在不改变Sharpe的情况下提供凸性与更浅的回撤。

Conclusion: 基于价格历史的简单马尔可夫链排名框架可以替代传统均值-方差优化，实现更优的风险调整后收益。波动率排名具有可预测性而收益排名几乎不可预测，但结合两者构建的组合在样本外表现优异，说明该框架具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We present a simple framework for dynamic portfolio management that uses nothing but daily prices, trading volumes, and market capitalizations. Its state is three fixed-size matrices built from the price history: the distance matrix of the return correlations and the transition matrices of two Markov chains that rank the S\&P 500 names monthly by trailing return and by trailing volatility. These three matrices rest on the price history alone, the same information Markowitz mean-variance optimization draws on, but they replace its expected-return vector and covariance matrix. Our method requires no matrix inversion, works on outlier-robust cross-sectional ranks, and is dynamic rather than single-period. Empirically the volatility rank is forecastable one step ahead while the return rank stays close to unforecastable. A portfolio built on the forecasts, a market-neutral momentum long-short blended with an opportunistic long-only sleeve, beats the market on two non-overlapping out-of-sample test sets, January 2022 to December 2024 and January 2025 to July 2026, at Sharpes of $1.06$ and $1.32$ against the market's $0.78$ and $1.14$, respectively, net of a five-basis-point trading cost and marked to market daily. It also outperforms the classical minimum-variance and maximum-diversification portfolios. Diversifying the long sleeve by residual distance adds a further edge on both periods, lifting the Sharpe to $1.08$ and $1.44$ and the annualized return from $18\%$ to $20\%$ and from $44\%$ to $56\%$, respectively. A convex information-leader overlay separately insures the market-neutral sleeve, buying convexity and a shallower drawdown at a small cost in return, the Sharpe unchanged.

</details>

### [75] [Recursive transformers for semiconductor thermo-mechanical reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27251)
*Kart-leong Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对工程设计中基于Transformer的代理模型参数过多、容易过拟合的问题，提出并系统评估了三种递归Transformer范式（Tiny递归模型、深度递归Transformer、简单递归Transformer），在热机械可靠性分析和Laplace PDE求解两个低维工程任务上验证了递归权重共享机制能有效平衡预测精度、参数效率和计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer架构在工程设计空间中往往过度参数化——工程设计数据集通常小且低维，大规模仿真数据生成成本高昂，过多的参数容量导致过拟合而非性能提升，同时带来不必要的内存和计算开销。因此需要转向"增加计算而非增加可学习参数"的架构设计思路。

Method: 提出并对比三种递归Transformer范式：a) Tiny递归模型、b) 提出的深度递归Transformer、c) 简单递归Transformer。通过递归权重共享减少可学习参数、增加计算深度，并进行硬件感知的评估，比较其预测性能（Recall、MRR）、参数量和计算复杂度（FLOPs）。在两个低维工程预测任务上验证：先进半导体封装的热机械可靠性分析（热循环下应力和翘曲的FEA代理）以及Laplace PDE电容场迭代数值求解。

Result: 实验结果表明，递归权重共享Transformer在预测精度、参数效率和计算成本之间提供了有效且可泛化的权衡，适用于小数据工程代理建模场景。

Conclusion: 递归Transformer（特别是通过权重共享实现的深度递归结构）是资源受限条件下工程代理建模的有效架构选择，论文提供了选择递归Transformer架构的实用设计指南。

Abstract: Transformer-based surrogate models are increasingly used to replace expensive first-principles simulation in engineering design. But conventional transformer architectures are often over parameterized for the small, low-dimensional datasets typical of engineering design spaces, where large simulation data is expensive to generate. Under these conditions, excess parameter capacity leads to overfitting rather than improved accuracy, while also incurring unnecessary memory and compute overhead. This motivates a shift towards architectures that focus on additional compute rather than additional learnable parameters. This paper presents a hardware-aware evaluation of three recursive transformer paradigms for surrogate thermo-mechanical analysis of advanced packages: a)Tiny Recursive Model, b) our proposed Depth Recursive transformer, c) and a simple recursive transformer. We systematically compare their predictive performance (Recall, Mean Reciprocal Rank), parameter count, computational complexity (FLOPs), providing practical design guidelines for selecting recursive transformer architectures under resource-constrained scenarios. We validate this principle on two low-dimensional engineering prediction tasks: 1) thermo-mechanical reliability analysis of advanced semiconductor packages, where stress and warpage from thermal cycling must be evaluated repeatedly across a design-of-experiments sweep under costly finite element analysis (FEA). 2) Laplace PDE iterative numerical solver for capacitance field. Overall, recursive weight-sharing transformers provide an effective and generalizable trade-off between prediction accuracy, parameter efficiency, and computational cost for small data engineering surrogate modeling.

</details>


### [76] [Regularizing modality contribution drift in multimodal continual learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27260)
*Zhen Zhang,Jielei Chu,Bin Liu,Tianrui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模态贡献漂移（MCD）这一新问题，指多模态持续学习中各模态相对贡献及交互作用在增量任务间发生漂移。作者提出 CMCDR 正则化方法，通过保留旧任务的模态贡献结构来缓解遗忘，包含基于回放和无回放两种版本，在多个多模态持续学习基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MMCL 方法主要关注跨模态表示对齐或语义相似性，忽视了各模态相对贡献及其交互作用在增量任务间的稳定性。这种决策层面的漂移（MCD）未被现有方法显式处理，导致多模态持续学习中的知识遗忘问题未能根本解决。

Method: 1) 定义 MCD 分数，通过对模态子集进行受控干预，结合贡献强度和相对依赖变化来量化模态贡献漂移。2) 提出 CMCDR 正则化方法，包含两个版本：回放版本使用模态子集干预作为诊断探针作用于存储的旧样本，比较当前模型与冻结旧模型的贡献轮廓并约束变化；无回放版本使用当前任务样本作为探针，蒸馏冻结模型对旧任务的贡献响应。

Result: 在模态类增量学习和持续视觉问答任务上进行实验，验证了 CMCDR 的通用性和有效性。理论和经验分析还解释了为什么现有 MMCL 方法无法可靠缓解 MCD。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统关注多模态持续学习中的决策层模态贡献漂移问题，提出的 CMCDR 方法通过显式约束模态贡献结构，有效缓解了多模态知识遗忘，为 MMCL 提供了新的研究视角和解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal continual learning (MMCL) aims to learn emerging knowledge from multimodal data while preserving knowledge. To mitigate forgetting, current MMCL methods usually focus on cross-modal representation alignment or semantic similarity, but they overlook whether the relative contributions of individual modalities and their interactions remain stable across incremental tasks. We term this decision-level shift Modality Contribution Drift (MCD) and quantify it with the MCD score, which combines contribution-strength and relative-reliance changes under controlled interventions on modality subsets. Theoretical and empirical analyses further explain why current MMCL methods cannot reliably mitigate this drift. To this end, we propose Continual Modality Contribution Drift Regularization (CMCDR), which preserves the modality contribution structure of previously learned tasks. Since MMCL settings differ in whether old exemplars are available, CMCDR includes both replay-based and replay-free versions. The replay-based version uses modality-subset interventions as diagnostic probes on stored old samples, compares their contribution profiles between the current model and a frozen previous model, and constrains changes in old-sample modality-specific and interaction contributions. The replay-free version uses current-task samples as probes and distills the frozen model's old-task contribution responses, thereby regularizing the observed contribution profile without exemplars. Experiments on multimodal class-incremental learning and continual visual question answering validate the generality and effectiveness of CMCDR.

</details>


### [77] [DoTime: A Synthetic Benchmark Generator for Interventional and Counterfactual Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27263)
*Dennis Thumm,Billy Tim Anthony,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DoTime，一个开放、可扩展且具有理论基础的多元时间序列结构因果模型（TSCM）生成器，支持干预和反事实采样，并发布了dotime PyPI包及四个冻结的评测套件。该生成器弥补了现有因果推断基准在小规模、观测性、领域限定方面的不足，特别支持连续时间干预窗口、反事实采样、机制切换SCM和非平稳动力学。作者还展示了该生成器可作为因果基础模型的先验，并验证了干预训练相比纯观测模型在方向准确率上具有可测优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列因果推断基准大多是观测性的、小规模的或领域特定的，在医疗、政策评估和气候科学等关键领域中对干预和反事实估计的支持严重不足。缺乏一个可扩展、通用且具有真实因果结构的合成数据生成器，阻碍了干预性和反事实因果推断方法的发展与评测。

Method: DoTime引入了一个多元时间序列结构因果模型（TSCM）生成器，其核心特性包括：连续时间干预窗口、带积极性保护的反事实采样模式、作为中断时间序列严格推广的机制切换SCM、通过切换SCM参数构造的非平稳动力学、以及将趋势和结构断裂置于评估窗口内的确定性斜坡和正弦干预轮廓。生成器发布为PyPI包（dotime），包含四个冻结的评估套件，涵盖10万条轨迹的训练规模快照和八个命名识别结构，每种结构均有精确的真实因果标注，包括配对干预轨迹和共享噪声反事实。作者还实现了一个因果基础模型参考实现（PFN），并通过跨三种训练种子的系统对比验证了干预训练的优势。

Result: 在结构匹配的留出集评估中，使用干预先验拟合的网络（PFN）在方向准确率上相比同容量观测模型获得了正的差距，该结果在每一种结构、轨迹长度和训练种子下均保持一致，证明了干预训练的因果方向学习优势具有可复现性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DoTime作为一个开放、可扩展且理论完备的时间序列因果生成器，填补了干预性和反事实时间序列基准的空白；同时通过实证验证了干预性训练能为因果基础模型带来可测量的方向准确率优势，为该领域提供了可证伪的基准声明和稳定的评测基础设施。

Abstract: Most benchmarks for causal inference over time series are observational, small, or domain-specific, leaving interventional and counterfactual estimation under-served exactly where it matters most, such as in healthcare, policy evaluation, and climate science. We introduce \textbf{DoTime}, an open, scalable, and theoretically grounded generator of multivariate temporal structural causal models (TSCMs) with interventions, released as the \code{dotime} PyPI package together with four frozen evaluation suites. Beyond existing work, it adds capabilities absent from prior generators: continuous-time intervention \emph{windows}, counterfactual sampling modes with a positivity guard, regime-switching SCMs as a strict generalization of interrupted time series, non-stationary dynamics by construction with switching SCM parameters, and deterministic ramp and sinusoidal intervention profiles that place trends and structural breaks \emph{inside} the evaluation window. Moreover, it demonstrates the suitability of the generator as a prior for a causal foundation model reference implementation. The released suites span a training-scale snapshot of $100{,}000$ trajectories and eight named identification structures, each with exact ground truth: paired interventional trajectories from the same SCM throughout, and shared-noise counterfactuals in the continuous-time suite. We ship reference baseline implementations with an evaluation harness, and pose a falsifiable claim: interventional training buys a measurable direction-accuracy advantage over an observational model of identical capacity. It is tested across three training seeds per arm. Under structure-matched evaluation on held-out episodes, the interventional prior-fitted network's (PFN) gap is positive in every structure, trajectory length, and seed tested.

</details>


### [78] [PlatformBid: An Auto-Bidding Benchmark from a Unified Advertising Platform's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27265)
*Shengtian Yang,Yewen Li,Peng Jiang,Zhiyi Lyu,Bo An,Peng Jiang,Qingpeng Cai,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PlatformBid，这是首个从统一广告平台视角设计的实时竞价（RTB）综合基准测试框架，涵盖同质竞争、异质竞争和促销竞争三种代表性场景，系统评估了多种现有自动竞价方法，并提出基于流匹配（flow-matching）的新方法BidFlow。在线实验（快手平台）显示目标成本提升0.68%，验证了离线-在线一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动竞价算法仅关注需求方平台（DSP）侧，以最大化广告主转化率为目标。然而，当前大型广告平台（如社交媒体和电商公司）已内部整合SSP、DSP和Ad Exchange功能。从平台视角看，自动竞价算法的目标不仅是最大化广告主转化率，还要最大化平台总营收。现有自动竞价研究缺乏平台中心化的评估框架，亟需新的基准来推动该领域发展。

Method: (1) 提出PlatformBid，第一个从统一广告平台视角设计的综合基准，定义三种代表性场景：同质竞争（所有广告主使用相同算法）、异质竞争（使用多样化算法策略）、促销竞争（部分广告主在促销活动期间激增预算）。(2) 系统评估多种现有自动竞价方法，涵盖经典控制方法、基于强化学习的方法和生成式方法。(3) 提出基于流匹配（flow-matching）的新方法BidFlow，利用流匹配方法的表达性策略表示来应对动态竞争环境。

Result: 在快手上的在线实验显示BidFlow在目标成本上获得+0.68%的改进，为PlatformBid的离线-在线一致性提供了部署证据。

Conclusion: PlatformBid填补了平台中心化自动竞价评估框架的空白，为自动竞价研究提供了更贴近真实场景的基准环境；BidFlow验证了流匹配方法在动态竞价环境中的有效性。

Abstract: Real-time bidding is central to computational advertising, comprising three elements: Supply Side Platform (SSP) selling ad impressions, Demand Side Platform (DSP) bidding for advertisers, and Ad Exchange conducting auctions between them. Traditional auto-bidding algorithms focus solely on the DSP side, maximizing advertiser conversions by adjusting bids against competitors. However, current big ad platforms, such as social media and e-commerce companies, now integrate SSP, DSP, and Ad Exchange functions internally. From such ad platforms' perspective, the goal of the auto-bidding algorithms is not only to maximize the advertisers' conversions, but also the total revenue of the platform. Given the lack of platform-centric evaluation frameworks and the pressing need to advance auto-bidding research, we propose PlatformBid - the first comprehensive benchmark designed from a unified ad platform's perspective. To accurately reflect the real-world auto-bidding scenarios, we define three representative settings: (1) homogeneous competition with identical algorithms across advertisers, (2) heterogeneous competition with diverse algorithmic strategies, and (3) promotional competition where some advertisers surge budgets for boosting sales during promotional events like Black Friday. We systematically evaluate a broad spectrum of existing auto-bidding methods across these settings, encompassing classical control methods, RL-based methods, and recent generative methods. Besides these methods, we further propose a novel auto-bidding method based on flow-matching, termed BidFlow, which leverages the flow-matching method's expressive policy representation to effectively handle dynamic competitive environments. Online experiments on Kuaishou further show a +0.68\% improvement in target cost, providing deployment evidence for the offline-online consistency of PlatformBid.

</details>


### [79] [Beyond KV Reconstruction: Functional Reconstruction for MLA Draft Models in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27269)
*Weiye Shi,Fanxu Meng,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端（E2E）的功能重构方法，用于将现有的MHA/GQA模型转换为MLA架构，以在保持投机解码（speculative decoding）的草稿-验证一致性（draft-token acceptance）的同时，获得MLA的KV缓存压缩和内存带宽优势。该方法在转换后对每个MLA注意力模块进行优化，使其在标定隐状态上复现原始MHA/GQA模块经过输出投影后的响应，从而避免直接转换带来的注意力函数误差。实验覆盖192种配置，验证了该方法在37/64的任务单元中显著提升接受率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLA因其紧凑的潜在状态替代不断增长的KV缓存，对长上下文LLM推理至关重要，能显著降低解码内存流量。但目前大多数强大的开源检查点使用MHA/GQA架构，需要转换为MLA以获得缓存效率而不需从头训练。然而，直接的MHA/GQA-to-MLA转换会导致低秩分解和RoPE处理带来的注意力函数误差，虽然在独立生成时可容忍，但会显著降低投机解码中草稿token的接受率。因此需要一种转换方法在获取MLA缓存效率的同时，保持草稿与目标验证之间的一致性。

Method: 本文将MLA草稿构建重新定义为功能重构而非缓存压缩问题。提出的端到端（E2E）方法对每个转换后的MLA注意力模块进行优化，使其在标定（calibration）隐状态上精确复现原始MHA/GQA对应模块在输出投影（post-output-projection）后的响应。该方法与具体转换器（converter-agnostic）无关，作为转换后的后处理步骤，保持已转换的缓存和推理图不变，且不需要验证器（verifier）的logits或监督信号。

Result: 研究者在192种模型-转换器-后端-任务配置上进行了评估，覆盖四组Llama/Qwen草稿-目标模型对、TransMLA和MHA2MLA两种转换器、HF和vLLM两个推理后端，以及四个包含200个提示词的评测任务。在0.5个百分点的报告容差下，功能重构方法在64个匹配任务单元中有37个实现了实质性改善，26个基本不变，仅1个出现实质性下降。

Conclusion: 功能重构方法能够在不改变推理图和缓存结构的前提下，显著缓解直接MHA/GQA-to-MLA转换对投机解码草稿接受率的负面影响，是一种有效的转换器无关的后处理优化手段。该工作为在现有模型上获得MLA缓存效率与投机解码加速的兼容兼顾提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multi-head latent attention (MLA) is increasingly important for long-context LLM inference because compact latent states replace the growing key-value (KV) cache and reduce decoding memory traffic. Yet most capable open checkpoints use multi-head or grouped-query attention (MHA/GQA), so conversion is needed to obtain MLA's cache efficiency without retraining from scratch. Speculative decoding offers complementary acceleration, but its speedup depends on agreement between draft proposals and target verification. We find that direct MHA/GQA-to-MLA conversion can sharply reduce this agreement: low-rank factorization and RoPE handling introduce attention-function errors that may be tolerable for standalone generation but substantially lower draft-token acceptance. We therefore formulate MLA draft construction as functional reconstruction rather than cache compression. Our end-to-end (E2E) method optimizes each converted MLA attention module to reproduce the post-output-projection response of its original MHA/GQA counterpart on calibration hidden states. This converter-agnostic post-conversion procedure preserves the converted cache and inference graph and requires neither verifier logits nor verifier supervision. We evaluate 192 model-converter-backend-method-task configurations spanning four Llama/Qwen draft-target pairs, TransMLA and MHA2MLA, HF and vLLM, and four 200-prompt tasks. With a 0.5-percentage-point reporting tolerance, Functional Reconstruction materially improves acceptance in 37 of 64 matched task cells, leaves 26 practically unchanged, and materially decreases one. Code and evaluation artifacts are available at https://github.com/swyhahaha/FunctionalMLA.

</details>


### [80] [RLPF: Reinforcement Learning from Performance Feedback for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27271)
*Huihao Jing,Haozhe Cui,Wenbin Hu,Shaojin Chen,Haochen Shi,Changxuan Fan,Yuxuan Liu,Hanyu Yang,Sirui Zhang,Ziyi Chen,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RLPF（基于性能反馈的强化学习）方法，将执行结果转化为分阶段奖励信号：失败程序按执行进度排序，正确程序按相对于基线和专家参考的效率提升排序，从而训练代码智能体偏好更快的正确实现。在PerfCodeBench上，Qwen3-32B的正确且可运行方案从11.1%提升至54.6%，相对效率从8.1%提升至38.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码模型的训练信号大多止步于正确性，忽略了运行效率这一关键维度。对于系统代码，两个程序可能通过相同测试但运行时间差异巨大。然而运行时作为奖励信号存在脆弱性：仅在程序正确后才有意义、跨任务差异大、且当多数采样程序无法编译或运行时几乎无指导作用。因此需要设计一种能够利用执行反馈来引导模型优化程序效率的训练方法。

Method: RLPF将执行结果转化为分阶段奖励（staged reward）：对于失败的程序，按其执行进度排序，在正确性达成前提供有用的梯度信号；对于正确的程序，按其对基线的相对改进（相对于专家参考）排序，提供性能敏感的训练信号。该方法在Qwen3-32B上微调，使用PerfCodeBench作为训练基准。

Result: 在PerfCodeBench上，RLPF将正确且可运行的解决方案从11.1%提升到54.6%，相对效率从8.1%提升到38.6%。训练后的模型与更强的开源系统模型相比具有竞争力，其优化行为能适度迁移到EffiBench-X。附加研究表明，模型生成的参考能提供有用但较弱的监督信号，完整的复合奖励比仅正确性或仅运行时基线的奖励更可靠。

Conclusion: 结果表明代码智能体不仅可以通过训练学会通过测试，还可以学会优化其编写的程序。RLPF的分阶段奖励设计有效克服了运行时作为奖励信号的脆弱性问题，为一进步用执行反馈训练高效代码模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Code models are increasingly trained with execution feedback, but most training signals still stop at correctness. This leaves an important gap for systems code: two programs can pass the same tests while differing greatly in runtime. We study how to train code agents to prefer faster correct implementations, rather than treating efficiency only as an evaluation metric. The key difficulty is that runtime is a fragile reward. It is meaningful only after a program is correct, varies across tasks, and gives little guidance when most sampled programs fail to compile or run. We propose \textbf{RLPF}, reinforcement learning from performance feedback, which turns execution outcomes into a staged reward. Failed programs are ordered by execution progress, while correct programs are ranked by their relative improvement from the baseline toward the expert reference. This gives useful feedback before correctness and performance-sensitive feedback after correctness. Fine-tuning Qwen3-32B with RLPF on PerfCodeBench raises correct-and-runnable solutions from $11.1\%$ to $54.6\%$ and improves relative efficiency from $8.1\%$ to $38.6\%$. The trained model becomes competitive with stronger open-weight systems, and its optimization behavior transfers modestly to EffiBench-X. Additional studies show that model-generated references provide useful but weaker supervision, and that the full composite reward is more reliable than correctness-only or runtime-only baselines. These results suggest that code agents can be trained not only to pass tests, but also to optimize the programs they write.

</details>


### [81] [SDO: Structure-Aware Data Organization for Efficient LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27273)
*Jinliang Gao,Ning Yang,Hai Wang,Baili Xiao,Pin Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SDO（结构感知数据组织），一种即插即用的数据组织框架，通过暴露驱动的反馈机制，利用外部嵌入空间的结构信息动态组织小批量组成和样本暴露，解决大模型后训练中静态数据组织导致样本训练不均匀的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型后训练成本高昂，现有效率优化主要聚焦于样本选择或训练调度，但数据组织本身通常是静态预处理步骤，基于嵌入的分组方法在训练前构建固定分区，无法适应训练中样本暴露的动态变化，导致所有样本暴露程度相似而优化需求不同，造成部分样本冗余更新、部分样本训练不足。

Method: SDO框架包含两大机制：(1) 局部感知批处理（locality-aware batching）：通过KNN邻域遍历在冻结的外部嵌入上构建连贯的小批量；(2) 跨轮次的暴露均衡调度（exposure-balanced scheduling）：记录每个样本的参与次数，降低过度暴露样本的采样概率以保持长期覆盖。SDO逐轮次在冻结的外部嵌入上运行，无需模型预热训练开销。

Result: 在SFT、DPO和GRPO三种训练范式下，SDO均加速了收敛，在训练早期到中期阶段的加速效果最为显著，产生更连贯的梯度，并在不同类型的问题上实现更均衡的准确率，且不会永久排除训练样本。

Conclusion: SDO通过结构感知的数据组织与暴露驱动的动态调度，在不增加模型训练开销的前提下有效提升了大模型后训练的效率与质量，是一种实用的即插即用方案。

Abstract: Post-training of large language models is expensive, and existing efficiency improvements mainly focus on selecting informative samples or designing training schedules. However, data organization itself is usually treated as a static preprocessing step: embedding-based grouping methods construct fixed partitions before training and cannot adapt to the evolving sample exposure during optimization. As a result, all samples receive similar exposure despite their different optimization needs, leading to redundant updates for some samples while leaving others under-optimized. To address this problem, we propose SDO (Structure-Aware Data Organization), a plug-and-play data organization framework with an exposure-driven feedback mechanism that organizes mini-batch composition and sample exposure according to representation-space structure. SDO operates epoch by epoch on frozen external embeddings, avoiding model warm-up training overhead: within each epoch, locality-aware batching forms coherent mini-batches via KNN neighborhood traversal; across epochs, exposure-balanced scheduling records per-sample participation and reduces the sampling probability of over-exposed samples to preserve long-term coverage. Across SFT, DPO, and GRPO, SDO accelerates convergence, with the largest gains observed in the early-to-mid phase, producing more coherent gradients and more balanced accuracy across question types without permanently excluding training samples.

</details>


### [82] [Rethinking EEG-Based Disease Diagnosis: Decoupling Instance Representation Learning from Subject-Level Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27274)
*Zhiyuan Ma,Zeyuan Li,Zhiyi Lu,Jiacheng Hao,Youlang Du,Zhen Jiang,Xinche Zhang,Yuhao Sun,Sen Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BridgeMIL，一种两阶段框架用于EEG疾病诊断。第一阶段通过时间对齐和子袋对齐预训练编码器，避免继承实例标签；第二阶段将编码器迁移到注意力MIL聚合器，仅对受试者预测施加监督，并通过特征保持限制表示漂移。在三个EEG疾病数据集和五个骨干网络上，BridgeMIL在15个设置中14个取得最高平均准确率，整体平均准确率76.57%，比最强基线高4.28个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG疾病诊断需要在受试者级别做出预测，但常见方法将记录分段为短实例并继承受试者标签，假设所有实例提供同等可靠的诊断证据，这并不成立。端到端MIL虽避免了继承标签的问题，但EEG数据集中受试者远少于实例，限制了表示学习的质量。

Method: BridgeMIL采用两阶段框架：阶段1在无继承实例标签的情况下预训练编码器，通过对齐时间相邻窗口和独立采样的受试者内子袋进行学习，并使用方差和协方差正则化防止坍塌、减少冗余（无需负样本对）；阶段2将编码器迁移到基于注意力的MIL聚合器，仅在受试者预测上施加监督，并通过特征保留机制限制表示漂移。

Result: 在三个EEG疾病数据集和五个代表性骨干网络上，BridgeMIL在15个数据集-骨干设置中的14个取得最高平均准确率，整体平均准确率为76.57%，比最强基线提高4.28个百分点。进一步分析显示：实例间继承标签可靠性差异显著；对受试者稀缺性比对实例稀缺性更敏感；表示空间更具结构性，形成清晰的受试者级簇和更好的诊断类别分离。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在利用丰富EEG实例的同时，将监督与受试者级预测目标对齐、避免给单个实例分配疾病标签，对提升EEG疾病诊断性能至关重要。

Abstract: EEG-based disease diagnosis requires one prediction per subject, yet common pipelines segment recordings into short instances, inherit the subject label for every instance, and train instance-level classifiers. This assumes that all instances provide equally reliable diagnostic evidence. Multiple instance learning (MIL) avoids inherited labels by treating each subject as a bag. However, EEG datasets contain far fewer subjects than instances, which can limit the quality of the representations learned by end-to-end MIL. We propose BridgeMIL, a two-stage framework that decouples instance representation learning from subject-level supervision. Stage 1 pretrains the encoder without inherited instance labels by aligning temporally nearby windows and independently sampled within-subject sub-bags. Variance and covariance regularization prevent collapse and reduce redundancy without negative pairs. Stage 2 transfers the encoder to an attention-based MIL aggregator, applies supervision only to subject predictions, and limits representation drift through feature retention. Across three EEG disease datasets and five representative backbones, BridgeMIL attains the highest mean accuracy in 14 of 15 dataset-backbone settings and an overall mean accuracy of 76.57%, 4.28 percentage points higher than the strongest baseline. Further analyses reveal substantial variation in inherited-label reliability across instances, greater performance sensitivity to subject scarcity than to instance scarcity, and a more structured representation space with distinct subject-wise clusters and improved separation between diagnostic classes. Together, these findings underscore the importance of aligning supervision with the subject-level prediction objective while learning from abundant EEG instances without assigning disease labels to individual instances.

</details>


### [83] [Flat Score, Amplified Failures: How the Error Budget Masks Damage in Quantized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27275)
*Jiwon Jang,Kisu Yang,Heuiseok Lim,Hyunwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验了4-bit权重量化在多轮工具调用智能体场景下是否真的近乎无损。研究发现，虽然标准指标（任务得分）看似不受影响，但量化会放大模型已有的失败模式（如工具名幻觉），只是被基准的十个错误预算所掩盖；当收紧预算时，量化造成的分数差距会重新暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4-bit权重量化被广泛报道为近乎无损，但现有评估可能只关注标准指标而忽略了过程层面的影响。随着多轮工具调用智能体成为量化最关键的落地场景，有必要检验这一宣称在该场景下是否成立。

Method: 在τ²-bench基准上，对两个开放权重模型家族（含dense和MoE变体）和两个领域（共八个单元，每个456个episode），在16/8/4-bit三档精度下进行量化评估。除标准任务得分外，还引入了过程级诊断：每通道错误率、多重比较校正、等价性检验，以及紧缩错误预算（从十个减到两个）的敏感性分析，并用定向错误修复提示验证归因。

Result: 标准指标下量化看似无损（无单元格分数变化通过多重比较校正，最大损伤单元的等价性检验将变化限定在±7.5分内）。但量化将已有失败（电信领域工具名幻觉、零售领域实体错误）放大至2.5倍（每任务+17.6分），且不产生新失败（失败集秩相关≥0.94，仅0.18%新事件）。分数持平是因为十个错误预算吸收了额外失败；将预算缩至两个时，仅在量化添加了错误量的那个单元格暴露出17分的差距。定向修复提示能精确消除量化损伤。

Conclusion: 标准任务奖励指标在量化评估中具有掩盖作用，无法反映量化对失败模式的过程级放大。建议在报告任务奖励的同时，汇报每通道错误率和紧缩预算下的成功率作为补充诊断指标。

Abstract: Post-training quantization to 4-bit weights is widely reported to be nearly lossless. We test this claim for multi-turn, tool-calling agents, where it now matters most. On $τ^2$-bench, across two open-weight model families in dense and MoE variants and two domains (eight cells, 456 episodes each, at 16-, 8-, and 4-bit weights), quantization indeed looks free on the standard metric. No cell shows a score change that survives multiple-comparison correction, and in the cell that carries the largest process damage, equivalence testing bounds the change within $\pm$7.5 points. The process tells a different story. Quantization amplifies the failure the model already exhibits at full precision (tool-name hallucination in telecom, with the same directional trend in retail entity errors) by up to 2.5$\times$ in volume (+17.6 points per task), while creating essentially no new failures. The failure set is the same at every precision (rank correlation $\geq$ 0.94, 0.18% novel events). The score stays flat because the benchmark's ten-error budget absorbs the extra failures. Shrinking the budget to two errors re-exposes a score gap of 17 points, and it does so only in the one cell where quantization added error volume, exactly as the masking account predicts. A targeted error-repair prompt, run for five telecom models at every precision, removes the damage exactly and only where it lives. Both diagnostics, the per-channel error rate and success under a shrinking budget, come from logs benchmarks already collect; we suggest reporting them alongside task reward.

</details>


### [84] [Strategies for Milestone-driven Start-ups in Multi-activity Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27563)
*Zhengli Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究创业公司在里程碑导向环境下的最优策略选择问题。作者构建了一个扩散过程模型，公司状态由该过程刻画，创业者可在多个（≥3个）不同成本、不同漂移和方差的活动中选择。论文通过引入"有效前沿曲线"概念，用风险性（漂移-波动率比）和成本效益（漂移-成本比）两个直观指标对控制进行排序，并完全求解了最优策略。该研究的独特之处在于发现了有效前沿曲线可因模型参数不同而呈现不同类型，导致最优策略结构发生质性变化，这是首次在随机控制模型中发现这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创业公司在生存过程中需要跨越多个里程碑目标。然而，连续时间随机控制模型在控制数量≥3时通常极难求解，现有研究缺乏对多活动选择下最优策略的系统性分析。作者希望通过构建一个可完全求解的模型，为创业公司提供直观的评估指标和策略指导。

Method: 作者建立了一个以扩散过程刻画公司状态的连续时间随机控制模型。创业者可在多个控制中选择，每个控制具有不同的成本、漂移和方差。公司状态到达上边界即成功，到达下边界即失败。作者通过定义有效前沿曲线（按风险性和成本效益排序控制）来刻画最优策略结构，并针对不同参数情形下的前沿曲线类型进行了完整的策略求解。

Result: 论文完全求解了最优策略并给出了显式的策略结构表征。最优策略仅使用由有效前沿曲线确定的控制集合。研究发现，根据模型参数不同，有效前沿曲线可呈现不同类型，导致最优策略结构发生质性差异——这是首次在随机控制模型中发现有效前沿曲线存在多种类型的情况。

Conclusion: 该研究为创业公司提供了两种直观的评估指标（风险性和成本效益）来评价其活动选择，揭示了里程碑导向下最优策略如何随具体场景发生质性变化，为创业决策研究提供了基础性支撑。

Abstract: New venture start-ups need to ``survive'' through multiple stages of reaching milestone targets. We investigate the strategies for start-ups in a milestone-oriented setting. We examine a model of an entrepreneurial start-up firm, where its state is captured by a diffusion process. The entrepreneur can choose between multiple activities (or controls), which incur different cost and determine the drift and the variance of the process. Depending on whether the process reaches a fixed upper boundary or a lower one, the start-up firm succeeds or fails. Continuous-time stochastic models with multiple ($\ge 3$) controls are typically very challenging to deal with. In this work, we are able to completely solve for the optimal policy and provide an explicit characterization of its structure. In particular, the optimal policy only uses controls from a set characterized by a so-called efficient frontier curve that orders the controls by two intuitive measures: riskiness (drift-to-volatility ratio) and cost-effectiveness (drift-to-cost ratio). A unique feature of our model is that depending on the model parameters, the efficient frontier curves can be of different types, resulting in qualitatively different structures of the optimal policy. As far as we know, this is the first study that analyzes a stochastic control model which admits efficient frontier curves of different types. Our work provides start-up firms with intuitive measures to evaluate their activities and offers valuable insights on how the optimal strategies in a milestone-oriented setting change qualitatively contingent upon the specific scenario. We believe the results provide a foundational block in the study of entrepreneurial decision-making.

</details>


### [85] [The Role of Causality in Algorithmic Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28497)
*Srikanth Avasarala,Varun Gupta,Shahin Jabbari,Saber Salehkaleybar,Juba Ziani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个因果表现性框架（causal performative framework）来形式化算法救济（algorithmic recourse）的失败模式，指出仅翻转模型预测而不考虑真实资格改善的救济策略会导致策略博弈和预测精度下降。文章证明了因果结构感知的救济策略能在非凸优化问题下收敛到表现性稳定的均衡解，减少博弈激励，并在半合成和真实信用数据集上验证了优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法救济方法只关注翻转模型预测结果，不区分推荐的变化是否真正提升了个体资质，还是仅用于策略性博弈分类器。这导致部署的救济策略在模型重训练后失效，且引发行为响应降低预测精度。作者希望形式化这一失败模式并提出解决方案。

Method: 通过结构因果模型（SCM）建模救济动作如何在特征间传播并影响真实标签，将因果响应建模为非凸优化问题。作者刻画了表现性稳定解存在的条件，并分析了简单迭代动态下解的可计算性。核心思想是：相比忽略因果结构的救济策略（会引发大且错位的行为响应），因果救济能达到稳定均衡并减少博弈激励。

Result: 在半合成和真实信用数据集上的实验表明，所提出的因果救济方法始终优于标准经验风险最小化（ERM），并且减少了因策略性个体行为导致分布偏移而需要反复模型重训练的需求。

Conclusion: 因果感知的算法救济策略能够实现表现性稳定性，降低个体的博弈激励，从而在实际部署中更有效且更持久，减少了模型重训练的负担。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse aims to provide individuals with actionable changes to improve their predicted outcomes in high-stakes classification settings, such as loan and mortgage applications. However, most existing approaches focus only on flipping a model's prediction, without accounting for whether the recommended changes lead to genuine improvement in an individual's true qualifications or merely enable strategic gaming of the classifier. Consequently, deployed recourse policies can induce behavioral responses that degrade predictive accuracy and become ineffective after model retraining.
  In this work, we formalize this failure mode through a causal performative framework for recourse. We model how recourse actions propagate through a structural causal model, capturing interactions among features as well as their effect on the true label. These causal responses induce a non-convex optimization problem, even under standard convex losses. We characterize conditions under which performatively stable solutions exist and can be efficiently computed via simple iterative dynamics. Our analysis reveals that recourse policies that ignore causal structure can induce large, misaligned behavioral responses, whereas causal recourse leads to stable equilibria that reduce incentives for gaming. Experiments on both semi-synthetic and real credit datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms standard empirical risk minimization while reducing the need for repeated model retraining to accommodate distribution shifts caused by strategic agent behavior.

</details>


### [86] [THGFM: Dual-Branch Temporal Heterogeneous Graph Fusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27303)
*Yixin Peng,Diego Collarana,Er Jin,Stefan Decker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出THGFM，一个面向Web规模时序异构图的双路径融合模型。该模型通过共享空间时间注意力分支实现跨类型参数高效迁移，通过关系类型分区时间注意力分支实现关系感知特化，并用类型条件非竞争门控求和融合将两者整合。此外，引入旋转变换时间注意力，将相对时间直接编码进注意力分数中。在学术图基准上平均提升3.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序异构图需要同时建模跨类型结构异质性和交互的时间动态，但现有方法难以在参数高效的跨类型迁移与关系感知特化之间取得平衡，且通常仅将时间作为注意力核外的加性特征注入，未能将时间直接融入注意力机制。

Method: THGFM采用双路径架构：(1) 共享空间时间注意力分支，用于参数高效的跨类型迁移；(2) 关系类型分区时间注意力分支，用于关系感知特化。二者通过"双路径关系-共享融合"整合，具体实现为"类型条件非竞争门控求和融合"，为共享和特化分支分配独立的、类型条件的特征级门控，允许两个分支独立放大或抑制而无需零和竞争。此外引入"旋转变换时间注意力"，将query和key按相对时间半相旋转后再进行匹配，将相对时间直接编码进注意力分数。

Result: THGFM在学术图基准上持续优于基线图Transformer模型，六任务平均增益+3.25%，其中OAG-CS PV上相对提升+12.37%，PF-L2上+4.87%，PF-L1上+1.18%，OGBN-MAG、HTAG-ArXiv和HTAG-DBLP上分别提升+4.24%、+3.73%和+4.61%。

Conclusion: THGFM通过双路径架构和旋转变换时间注意力，有效解决了时序异构图中跨类型迁移与关系特化的矛盾，并将相对时间直接注入注意力内核，在Web规模学术图上取得一致且显著的性能提升，验证了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: Temporal heterogeneous graphs offer a natural abstraction for dynamic relational systems in which diverse node and relation types co-exist and evolve over time. Learning on such graphs requires jointly modeling cross-type structural heterogeneity and the temporal dynamics of interactions, yet existing methods still struggle to reconcile parameter-efficient cross-type transfer with relation-aware specialization, and typically inject time only as additive features outside the attention kernel. We propose \textbf{THGFM}, a web-scale temporal heterogeneous graph fusion model that addresses both limitations within a unified dual-path architecture. THGFM couples a \textit{Shared-Space Temporal Attention} branch for parameter-efficient cross-type transfer with a \textit{Relational Type-Partitioned Temporal Attention} branch for relation-aware specialization, and integrates them through \textit{Dual-Path Relational--Shared Fusion}, instantiated with \textit{Type-Conditioned Non-Competitive Gated Sum Fusion}: a adaptive mechanism that assigns independent, type-conditioned feature-wise gates to the shared and specialized branches, allowing both to be amplified or suppressed without zero-sum competition. To directly incorporate relative time into the attention score, THGFM further introduces \textit{Rotary Temporal Attention}, which rotates queries and keys by half-phases of relative time before matching. THGFM consistently outperforms baseline graph transformer models on academic graphs benchmarks, delivering a $+3.25\%$ six-task mean gain, with peak relative gains of $+12.37\%$ on OAG-CS PV, $+4.87\%$ on PF-$L_2$, and $+1.18\%$ on PF-$L_1$, and $+4.24\%$, $+3.73\%$, and $+4.61\%$ on OGBN-MAG, HTAG-ArXiv, and HTAG-DBLP, respectively.

</details>


### [87] [The Kinetics of Training: A Driven-Nucleation Rate Law for Emergence, Plasticity Loss, and Circuit Control in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27281)
*Lei Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出语言模型能力形成的核心瓶颈在于"无部分信用联合对齐"（no-partial-credit joint alignment）——能力电路的所有部分必须在单次随机尝试中同时对齐，缺少任何一部分都毫无价值。通过指纹实验（五部分电路缺三与三部分电路缺三等待时间相近）和Pythia模型上的消融实验（去除一个部分仅保留中位数17%能力，而随机非部件头保留100%）验证了这一机制。作者推导出速率方程（缺失部分数指数驱动），并证明正向外推（能力点火可预测、可触发）、后向延迟（保留能力学习延迟随等待增长直至永远无法点火）以及损伤定位与修复（重初始化query-key切片可恢复可学习性）。最后在受控门控注意力模型中证明机制，并完成SGD噪声的涨落-耗散测试与退火调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型能力是如何形成的，以及为什么能力会在某个时刻突然"出现"（emergent）。作者发现关键不在于电路的大小，而在于所有部件必须在一个随机尝试中同时对齐——"缺一不可"的联合对齐是能力形成中最核心的限速步骤，之前的理论无法解释这种非连续涌现现象。

Method: 1) 设计"无捷径"（shortcut-free）装置比较不同缺失部件数量的电路等待时间；2) 在Pythia模型上跨7种能力、3种规模进行消融实验，区分真实部件与随机非部件头的差异；3) 推导速率方程 site x attempts x drive x exp(-beta*K) - destruction，并用预注册冻结常数进行前向/后向/定位三种方向的应用；4) 在受控门控注意力模型中进行机制证明，验证占用（occupation）强制期限的后果；5) 对SGD噪声进行涨落-耗散测试，安装噪声并实施退火调度来熔化、固定电路。

Result: 前向：能力从基线平直状态可在选定步骤点火（10/10达标，0/12低于阈值），并可从先驱预测到达时间（6个留出模型5%中位数误差）。后向：保留能力的学习延迟随等待增长，超过临界步骤后永不点火，且验证损失平滑下降使标准监控失效。修复：重初始化query-key切片恢复可学习性（6/6），value切片无效（0/6）。机制证明在门控注意力模型中完成。

Conclusion: 能力形成由"所有电路部分一次性对齐"这一稀有事件控制，其障碍随缺失部件数增长。该框架支持前向预测、可控点火、损伤定位与修复，且SGD天然噪声不足需人工注入并退火。适用范围为transformer中的合取电路（至1.4B规模）。

Abstract: A capability appears in a language model when the last parts of its circuit align in one stochastic attempt, and getting all but one right is worth nothing. We show this no-partial-credit joint alignment is the rate-limiting step of capability formation. Two fingerprints: in a shortcut-free apparatus a five-part circuit missing three waits as long as a three-part circuit missing three (1.19-1.37), so the wait counts missing parts, not size; and on Pythia across seven capabilities and three scales, ablating one part leaves a median 17% of the capability in 32 of 32 discriminating cells, where partial credit predicts 50-83% (p = 2e-10), while a random non-part head leaves 100%.
  One rare event whose barrier grows with missing parts yields a rate equation -- sites x attempts x drive x exp(-beta*K), minus destruction -- read three ways, each preregistered with frozen constants. Forward: a capability flat at baseline ignites at a step of our choosing once the mix passes a concentration floor (10/10 above, 0/12 below), and while still flat its arrival is datable from its precursor to 5% median error on six held-out models. Backward: the delay to learn a withheld capability grows with waiting until, past a critical step, it never ignites -- yet validation loss falls smoothly throughout, so standard monitors are blind to it. We locate the damage (heads commit to the base data) and isolate the cure: re-initializing only the query-key slices restores learnability (6/6) while the value slices do nothing (0/6). We prove the mechanism in a controlled gated-attention model: occupation forces a deadline whose consequences need no mixing assumption. Completed: SGD's noise fails the fluctuation-dissipation test, so we install one and anneal, melt and pin circuits on schedule. Scope: conjunction circuits in transformers to 1.4B.

</details>


### [88] [Benchmarking the Residual: What Long-Horizon Evaluations Add Beyond Matched Short-Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27283)
*Chao Peng,Zhiheng Lyu,Peijie Dong,Hande Dong,Qiang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"轨迹诱导退化"概念，指早期执行使后期任务变得更困难的现象，并主张衡量"长时程失败"时必须对比完整任务成功率与基于短阶段构建的基线预测，二者对数比率称为"时程残差"，以区分普通错误累积与任务本身因环境变化而变难的效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程基准中智能体表现随任务变长而下降，但现有观测无法解释失败根源：是普通错误在多阶段中累积，还是任务个体步骤本身更困难，抑或对话历史、工具输出、环境变化等累积使后续工作更难。作者希望建立一个严谨的框架来区分这些可能性。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文（position paper），提出方法论框架：1）定义"轨迹诱导退化"（trajectory-induced degradation）为早期执行使后期任务更困难；2）提出"时程残差"（horizon residual），即实际完整任务成功率与基于短个体阶段构建的基线预测之间的对数比率；3）强调对比需使用相同智能体配置，并预先规定阶段、检查点、信息和预算的选择方式。

Result: 论文提出用"时程残差"作为诊断工具来检验完整 rollout 是否偏离基线，但明确指出残差只能显示偏差的存在，仍需针对性的实验来解释偏差产生的原因。残差本身不足以说明退化机制。

Conclusion: 要声称存在"长时程失败"，基准必须满足严格的条件：对比实际成功率与由短阶段构建的基线预测，且所有实验条件需预先指定。该框架为长时程智能体评估提供了更严谨的方法论基础。

Abstract: Long-horizon benchmarks often show that agents fail more as tasks become longer. This observation is useful for deployment, but it does not by itself explain why failure occurs. More stages create more opportunities for ordinary errors to compound; longer tasks may also contain harder individual decisions or become harder as conversation history, tool outputs, and environment changes accumulate. We use trajectory-induced degradation to mean this last possibility: earlier execution makes later work harder. When the harmful accumulation is specifically the text visible to the model, it is often called context rot. In this position paper, we argue that to claim a "long-horizon failure", benchmarks must compare actual full-task success against a baseline prediction built from short, individual stages. We call the log-ratio between this prediction and actual success the horizon residual. The comparison must use the same agent configuration and specify in advance how stages, checkpoints, information, and budgets will be chosen. The residual shows that the full rollout differs from the chosen baseline; targeted experiments are still needed to explain why.

</details>


### [89] [Towards joint scaling laws with optimal batch size schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27731)
*Jiaxiang Li,Zhiqi Bu,Shiyun Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从凸优化的角度研究深度学习训练动态，推导出损失关于学习率和批次大小调度的联合刻画，进而提出闭式最优批次大小调度方案，并建立联合缩放定律，在大型语言模型训练中优于静态批次大小基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习通常在整个训练过程中保持批次大小不变，忽视了学习率和批次大小对训练动态的联合影响。这可能导致训练效率不高，因此需要研究两者共同作用的机制。

Method: 通过凸优化的视角分析深度学习训练动态，推导出损失函数关于学习率调度和批次大小调度的联合表征。基于这一表征，在给定学习率调度的情况下，推导出最优批次大小调度的闭式解，并进一步建立联合缩放定律。

Result: 提出的联合缩放定律在大型语言模型训练中一致性地优于静态批次大小基线，验证了动态批次大小调度的有效性。

Conclusion: 动态批次大小调度对于大规模语言模型训练具有重要意义，学习率和批次大小的联合调度策略能够显著提升训练效率。

Abstract: Modern deep learning typically keeps the batch size static throughout training, thus overlooking the joint effect of learning rate and batch size on the training dynamics. In this paper, we study the deep learning dynamics through the lens of convex optimization and derive a joint characterization of loss in terms of both schedules, applicable to general optimizers and model architectures. This characterization yields a closed-form optimal batch size schedule for any prescribed learning rate schedule, and further leads to joint scaling laws that consistently outperform static batch size baselines, highlighting the significance of dynamic batch size schedule in large language model training.

</details>


### [90] [TIER-MoE: Trust-Informed Expert Routing via Conditional Modality Risk for Multimodal Fusion in Biomedical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27289)
*Yu Chang,Anzhe Cheng,Chenwei Wu,Zhuoran Wang,Jiahao Chen,Tamoghna Chattopadhyay,Sophia I. Thomopoulos,Paul M. Thompson,Liyue Shen,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TIER-MoE是一个风险引导的子空间混合专家模型，通过定义样本特定的模态可靠性（即其单模态预测器预期损失的预测风险）来指导稀疏模态-专家路由，并在四个多模态生物医学数据集上优于现有方法，在预测性能和概率校准方面均取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态融合的核心承诺在于整合互补的证据来源，但更多证据并不总是带来更好的预测。现有融合方法（如丰富的跨模态交互和样本自适应融合）虽然取得了进展，但分配给某个模态的影响力并不能揭示该来源是否不可靠、冗余或与专用专家不匹配。因此需要一种能够明确建模样本特定模态可靠性的方法。

Method: TIER-MoE提出：(1) 将样本特定模态可靠性定义为该模态单模态预测器预计产生的预测损失（风险）；(2) 该风险通过袋外预测（out-of-fold predictions）学习，即由未在对应样本上训练的模型生成；(3) 将估计的风险与专家特定的子空间兼容性相结合，用于稀疏模态-专家路由；(4) 始终激活的共享路径保留多模态互补性。

Result: 在四个公共多模态生物医学数据集（涵盖阿尔茨海默病状态、皮肤病变恶性分类和视网膜分类）上，TIER-MoE在预测性能和概率校准方面优于现有最先进方法，Macro-F1和Brier分数均有一致改进，并在外部队列上展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: TIER-MoE通过风险引导的稀疏路由机制有效解决了模态不可靠、冗余或专家不匹配的问题，在生物医学多模态任务中取得了SOTA性能，同时具备良好的校准性和泛化能力。

Abstract: The promise of multimodal fusion lies in combining complementary sources of evidence, yet more evidence does not always yield a better prediction. Recent multimodal models have advanced fusion through richer cross-modal interaction and sample-adaptive fusion. However, the influence assigned to a modality during fusion does not reveal whether that source is unreliable, redundant, or poorly matched to a specialized expert. To address this limitation, we introduce TIER-MoE, a risk-guided subspace mixture-of-experts model that defines sample-specific modality reliability as the prediction loss its unimodal predictor is expected to incur. This risk is learned from out-of-fold predictions generated by models that were not trained on the corresponding sample. TIER-MoE combines the estimated risk with expert-specific subspace compatibility for sparse modality-expert routing, while an always-active shared path preserves multimodal complementarity. We evaluate TIER-MoE on four public multimodal biomedical datasets spanning Alzheimer's disease status, skin-lesion malignancy, and retinal classification. Results demonstrate its superiority over state-of-the-art methods in predictive performance and probability calibration, with consistent improvements in Macro-F1 and Brier score and strong zero-shot generalization to an external cohort.

</details>


### [91] [Encryption-Compatible Clustered Federated Learning via Distributed Expectation-Maximization over Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28338)
*Michael Ben Ali,Imen Megdiche,André Péninou,Olivier Teste*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FLAMECHE方法，将基于元数据的聚类联邦学习(CFL)重新形式化为分布式期望最大化(EM)过程，仅使用加法操作进行服务器更新，从而兼容标准联邦学习的隐私保护机制，在隐私、通信和计算效率的CFL三难困境中取得更好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类联邦学习方法面临隐私保护、通信成本和计算效率之间的权衡（即CFL三难困境）。基于元数据的聚类方法虽然通信和计算高效，但与标准FL隐私保护机制不兼容，限制了其实际应用。

Method: FLAMECHE将基于元数据的CFL重新设计为分布式期望最大化(EM)过程，将服务器端更新限制为仅加法操作，从而在不牺牲效率的前提下实现与加密安全FL方案的兼容性。

Result: 在多个数据集和多种异质性场景下的大量实验表明，FLAMECHE提升了客户端模型的有效性，实现了支持加密的元数据聚类方法，在CFL三难困境中优化了自身定位。

Conclusion: FLAMECHE成功解决了元数据聚类与隐私保护机制不兼容的问题，通过仅加法操作的设计实现了隐私兼容的元数据聚类，为聚类联邦学习在隐私保护场景下的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) addresses data heterogeneity in federated settings by grouping clients with similar data distributions to enable effective training. Existing methods face a trade-off between privacy preservation, communication cost, and computational efficiency. We formalize this as the CFL trilemma, according to which improving two of these dimensions comes at the expense of the third. A prominent paradigm relies on metadata (i.e., low-dimensional representations of client datasets shared with the server) to enable communication- and computation-efficient clustering. However, such approaches are not compatible with standard FL privacy-preserving mechanisms. To address this limitation, we propose FLAMECHE, which reformulates metadata-based CFL as a distributed Expectation-Maximization (EM) procedure, restricting server updates to additive operations while preserving efficiency. This design enables compatibility with practical secure FL schemes. We conducted extensive experiments on multiple datasets under various heterogeneous scenarios. Results show that FLAMECHE improves the effectiveness of client models. It enables encryption-compatible metadata-based clustering, enhancing its positioning within the CFL trilemma.

</details>


### [92] [EvoCause: LLM-Guided Evolution of Causal Graphs for Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27290)
*Lei Zan,Keli Zhang,Shifeng Xie,Jiale Zheng,Zehao Xiao,Zhiwei Dong,Ke Zhang,Ruichu Cai,Malik Tiomoko,Lujia Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoCause框架，利用LLM和专家标注来迭代优化因果图用于根因分析；同时发布TeleRCA基准数据集。EvoCause通过LLM提议图编辑，用确定性代码验证并保留最优图，在测试时无需调用LLM即可进行透明预测。实验表明该方法在合成数据和真实电信数据上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有根因分析方法从观测日志学习因果图，但学到的图是固定的，无法利用历史事件的专家诊断知识来持续改进。闭式循环需要一种方法，让专家标注能够约束图的结构，而不需要专家手动指定具体的边编辑操作。

Method: EvoCause框架：1）专家标注提供源节点约束；2）使用LLM提出语义上合理的图编辑候选；3）确定性代码验证节点身份和图的非循环性；4）在标注对齐集上保留最优图。测试时仅使用精炼后的图进行预测，无需LLM调用。同时使用PC因果发现算法初始化图结构。

Result: 在合成数据上，EvoCause相较于未精炼的PC基线，Node F1提升11.59个百分点，Case EM提升9.40个百分点，Graph F1提升4.59个百分点，nSHD降低0.2379。在TeleRCA数据集上，将人类可读的告警标题替换为匿名标识符导致Node F1和Case EM分别下降6.12和8.04个百分点，表明告警名称信息对图精炼贡献重要。

Conclusion: EvoCause成功通过LLM+专家标注闭环来改进根因分析的因果图，在不增加测试时推理成本的前提下显著提升性能。TeleRCA基准的发布为社区提供了专家标注的真实电信网络数据集，推动该领域研究。

Abstract: Modern telecommunication, cloud, and microservice systems emit correlated alarm cascades when components fail. Root cause analysis (RCA) aims to identify the small set of alarms that initiate each cascade. A common approach learns a causal graph from observational logs and predicts all zero-in-degree alarms in each incident-induced subgraph. However, the learned graph remains fixed and cannot benefit from expert diagnoses of historical incidents. We close this loop with EvoCause. Expert labels constrain which alarms should be source nodes but do not specify the edge edits needed to satisfy those constraints. EvoCause uses a large language model (LLM) to propose semantically plausible graph edits, while deterministic code validates node identities and acyclicity and retains the best graph on a labeled alignment set. At test time, the refined graph alone produces transparent predictions without an LLM call. We also release TeleRCA, an expert-annotated benchmark from a production telecommunication network containing $485{,}681$ alarm events spanning $194$ alarm types over $5{,}621$ resources. On synthetic data, EvoCause initialized with the PC causal discovery algorithm outperforms the unrefined PC baseline, raising Node F1, Case EM, and Graph F1 by $11.59$, $9.40$, and $4.59$ percentage points, respectively, while reducing nSHD by $0.2379$. On TeleRCA, replacing human-readable alarm titles with anonymous identifiers lowers Node F1 and Case EM by $6.12$ and $8.04$ percentage points, respectively, indicating that alarm-name information contributes to graph refinement.

</details>


### [93] [On-Policy and Off-Policy Learning for Large Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28408)
*Imad Aouali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了大规模动作空间下的上下文老虎机问题，涵盖在线策略学习和离线策略学习两个范式。在线部分提出了基于混合效应和扩散先验的结构化贝叶斯方法（meTS和dTS），离线部分提出了基于潜变量的结构化直接方法（sDM）、可高效优化的凹目标函数以及基于指数平滑和PAC-Bayesian界的可微悲观方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模动作空间中，无论是在线还是离线策略学习都面临严峻挑战：探索效率低下、数据覆盖稀疏、重要度采样权重方差高、外推偏差严重以及优化景观困难。现有方法难以在如此大的动作空间中有效共享信息并控制偏差-方差权衡。

Method: 论文提出两类方法：在线部分提出meTS（混合效应汤普森采样扩展）和dTS（利用扩散启发的先验建模动作间依赖关系）；离线部分提出sDM（基于潜变量的结构化直接方法）、新的策略加权对数似然凹目标函数，以及基于指数平滑和PAC-Bayesian界的可微悲观正则化重要度采样估计器。

Result: 在线方法通过动作间信息共享获得依赖有效动作数目的遗憾保证；离线方法证明了在大动作空间中优化误差可主导估计误差，并通过凹目标和悲观正则化方法有效控制偏差-方差权衡。

Conclusion: 论文系统性地解决了大规模动作空间下上下文老虎机在在线与离线两个范式中的核心挑战，为结构化信息共享和偏差-方差控制提供了理论保证与实用算法。

Abstract: This thesis studies policy learning in interactive systems where an agent observes a context, selects an action from a very large set, and receives partial feedback. The main framework is contextual bandits, with two paradigms: on-policy learning, where the agent interacts sequentially with the environment and minimizes regret, and off-policy learning, where it learns from logged data collected by a logging policy. In large action spaces, both settings face major challenges: inefficient exploration, sparse data coverage, high-variance importance weights, extrapolation bias, and difficult optimization landscapes. The first part develops structured Bayesian methods for on-policy learning. We introduce meTS, a mixed-effect extension of Thompson sampling, and dTS, which leverages diffusion-inspired priors to model dependencies between actions. These methods share information across actions and yield regret guarantees depending on an effective number of actions. The second part addresses off-policy learning. We propose sDM, a structured direct method based on latent variables, show that optimization error can dominate estimation error in large action spaces, and introduce concave, efficiently optimizable policy-weighted log-likelihood objectives. Finally, we develop differentiable pessimistic methods based on exponential smoothing and PAC-Bayesian bounds to control the bias-variance trade-off of regularized importance-sampling estimators.

</details>


### [94] [Generalization Bounds on Optimal Control for Transformer Training and Wasserstein Distributional Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27975)
*Kağan Akman,Naci Saldi,Serdar Yüksel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为使用动态规划递归训练的Transformer推导了有限样本泛化界。通过双重提升的测度值公式，将训练问题视为有限时域马尔可夫控制问题，并通过对量化模型的分析结合浓度不等式和Lipschitz稳定性估计，得到显式泛化界，最后将问题与Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在大规模应用中的成功与其理论理解之间存在差距，尤其是在使用动态规划式递归训练时的泛化保证方面。现有工作缺乏对这类训练过程的有限样本泛化界的严格分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1. 采用双重提升的测度值公式将Transformer动力学建模为概率定律；2. 将训练问题解释为有限时域马尔可夫控制问题；3. 通过量化状态、动作和测度状态空间得到量化模型；4. 利用有限度量空间上经验定律的浓度不等式结合值函数的Lipschitz稳定性估计推导显式有限样本泛化界；5. 通过显式近似误差将界传递回基础模型；6. 展示同一框架可导出分布鲁棒控制公式。

Result: 得到了Transformer在动态规划训练下的显式有限样本泛化界，并通过近似误差分析将其从量化模型转移到基础模型。此外，建立了Transformer泛化与Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化之间的理论联系。

Conclusion: 这项工作为理解Transformer的泛化能力提供了新的理论框架，将深度学习中的Transformer训练问题与最优控制理论和分布鲁棒优化建立了形式化联系，为后续的理论分析和算法改进奠定了基础。

Abstract: We derive finite-sample generalization bounds for Transformers trained with dynamic programming recursions. Building on the doubly lifted, measure-valued formulation of Transformer dynamics, we view data sets as probability laws on pairs of empirical input-output measures, allowing us to interpret the training problem as a finite-horizon Markovian control problem. We then analyze a quantized model, derived by quantizing the state, action, and measure-state spaces, and derive explicit finite-sample generalization bounds using concentration inequalities for empirical laws on finite metric spaces together with a Lipschitz stability estimate for the value function. These bounds are transferred to the base model at the cost of an explicit approximation error. Finally, we show that the same machinery yields a distributionally robust control formulation of the training problem, connecting Transformer generalization to Wasserstein distributionally robust optimization.

</details>


### [95] [Position, Not Provenance: Separating Reasoning Mediation from Sycophancy in Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27304)
*Supratik Bhowal,Subhrajyoti Basu,Aritra Gir Mahanta,Anik Pal Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoT-Mediate，一个行为学框架用于评估医学视觉语言模型（VLM）生成的思维链（CoT）推理是否真正因果性地影响其临床回答。通过双臂协议（重新提示vs前缀强制续写）和来源控制干预，在LLaVA-Med和MedGemma上各1000个VQA-RAD样本上进行评估，发现推理注入机制显著影响测量到的忠实度，上下文位置而非声明的来源是决定性因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学VLM会生成CoT推理后再回答临床问题，但现有工作未明确验证这些推理是否真正因果性地影响预测结果。需要区分推理真正介导了回答与模型因谄媚（sycophancy）而附和推理这两种情况，此前的框架未能有效解耦。

Method: 提出CoT-Mediate行为框架：(1) 双臂协议对比重新提示证据与前缀强制续写两种推理注入方式；(2) 来源控制干预（provenance-controlled intervention），保持推理内容相同但仅改变其归因来源，从而分离推理介导与谄媚效应。在LLaVA-Med和MedGemma上各评估1000个VQA-RAD样本。

Result: 前缀强制续写在两个模型上均比重新提示产生更高的介导忠实度。来源分析揭示了模型特有的服从行为差异。两个模型中，移除视觉证据会增加对注入推理的依赖，而侧别（laterality）是最不被忠实追踪的临床属性。

Conclusion: 推理注入机制的选择会显著影响测量到的推理忠实度；上下文位置（contextual position）而非声称的来源（provenance）是决定医学VLM是否使用其生成推理的主要因素。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) generate chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning before answering clinical questions, but whether this reasoning causally influences predictions remains unclear. We present CoT-Mediate, a behavioral framework that perturbs a single clinically meaningful attribute within a model's own generated reasoning and measures whether the resulting prediction follows the edited reasoning. Our framework combines a dual-arm protocol comparing re-prompted evidence with prefix-forced continuation, together with a provenance-controlled intervention that varies only the attributed source of identical reasoning to disentangle reasoning mediation from sycophancy. We evaluate LLaVA-Med and MedGemma on 1,000 VQA-RAD samples each. Prefix-forced continuation consistently yields higher mediation faithfulness than re-prompting, while the provenance analysis reveals distinct model-specific deference behaviors. Across both models, removing visual evidence increases reliance on injected reasoning, whereas laterality is the least faithfully tracked clinical attribute. These results show that the mechanism used to inject reasoning substantially affects measured faithfulness and that contextual position, rather than stated provenance, is the primary determinant of whether medical VLMs use their generated reasoning.

</details>


### [96] [ZUNA1.1: A more flexible EEG foundation model for Denoising and Super-resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27308)
*Christopher Warner,Jonas Mago,JR Huml,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ZUNA1.1，一个380M参数的扩散自编码器，用于灵活的脑电图（EEG）信号重建。该模型能够重建长达30秒的可变长度序列，支持任意数量的EEG通道、任意头皮位置，并能重建通道内的任意时间间隔。ZUNA1.1在性能上与早期ZUNA1模型相当，但灵活性大幅提升，且显著优于标准EEG去噪和重建方法（如MNE包中广泛使用的球面样条插值）。模型以Apache 2.0许可开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG信号重建方法（如球面样条插值）在灵活性上存在局限，难以处理任意通道配置、可变时间长度和部分通道内缺失的各种重建场景。作者旨在开发一个更灵活、通用的EEG重建模型，以覆盖广泛的重建任务需求。

Method: 采用380M参数的扩散自编码器架构（ZUNA1.1），设计上支持重建可变长度（最长30秒）的EEG序列、任意数量和位置的通道，以及通道内任意时间间隔的局部重建，在保持性能的同时大幅提升灵活性和任务适应性。

Result: ZUNA1.1在重建性能上与之前的ZUNA1模型相当，但在灵活性和任务覆盖范围上显著提升；同时继续大幅优于标准EEG重建方法如球面样条插值。

Conclusion: ZUNA1.1作为一个灵活且高性能的EEG重建模型，兼顾了性能与泛化能力，并以其开源许可（Apache 2.0）为EEG研究社区提供了可广泛使用的工具。

Abstract: We introduce ZUNA1.1, a 380M-parameter diffusion autoencoder for flexible EEG signal reconstruction. ZUNA1.1 is capable of reconstructing variable length sequences of up to 30s, with an arbitrary number of EEG channels at arbitrary scalp locations, and can reconstruct arbitrary temporal intervals within channels in addition to reconstructing entire channels. We demonstrate that ZUNA1.1 performs at least on par with our earlier ZUNA1 model, while being far more flexible and capable of handling a wide range of reconstruction tasks. ZUNA1.1 continues to substantially outperform standard EEG denoising and reconstruction methods such as spherical spline interpolation, which is ubiquitously deployed in the MNE package. The ZUNA1.1 model is released open source under the permissive Apache 2.0 license.

</details>


### [97] [Modeling Decisions in Blockchain Analytics: A Leakage-Aware Evaluation of Tree-Based vs. Sequential Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27350)
*Michał Bartnicki,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去泄漏的Sybil机器人检测框架，通过Blind-Spot协议消除高信号合约带来的标签泄漏，并使用Transaction Grammar表示钱包行为。在去泄漏评估下，XGBoost等树模型优于Transformer等序列模型，且延迟和能耗更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有采用深度学习将区块链活动视为准语言序列的Sybil检测方法存在两大问题：复杂序列模型计算成本高，难以用于实时监控；其报告的性能可能因高信号智能合约的标签泄漏而被夸大。因此需要回答：去泄漏后序列模型是否仍优于树模型、交易顺序与时机哪个信号更强、以及模型是否适合低延迟部署。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：1) Blind-Spot协议——消除与高信号合约相关的捷径/泄漏；2) Transaction Grammar表示——通过节奏（rhythm）、EVM执行结构和意图（intent）来建模钱包行为。评估方面，对比Transformer和BiLSTM序列模型与XGBoost和SVM基线，进行Ethereum行为体分类。

Result: 在去泄漏评估条件下，XGBoost在准确率上优于Transformer等序列模型，同时延迟更低、估算能耗更小。论文贡献了去泄漏的以太坊行为体分类框架以及Transaction Grammar钱包行为表示。

Conclusion: 去泄漏评估下，复杂的深度序列模型相对简单的树模型并无优势，反而在部署成本和效率上处于劣势。这提示该领域需要重新审视现有方法报告的准确性，并优先考虑轻量级、可实时部署的模型。

Abstract: Sybil bots are Ethereum actors that imitate legitimate users to extract airdrop rewards or influence governance. Recent Sybil detection methods increasingly use deep learning and treat blockchain activity as a quasi-linguistic sequence. However, complex sequence models are computationally expensive for real-time monitoring, and their reported performance may be inflated by label leakage from high-signal smart contracts. We ask whether and how organic users, Sybil bots, and MEV bots differ in the structural complexity of their transaction histories; whether sequential models outperform tree-based tabular models once leakage is reduced; whether transaction order or timing provides the stronger behavioral signal; and whether the resulting models are practical for low-latency deployment. Our approach to leakage-aware Sybil bot detection consists of a Blind-Spot protocol and a Transaction Grammar representation of wallet behavior. The former eliminates shortcuts associated with high-signal contracts, whereas the latter models wallets using rhythm, EVM execution structure, and intent. We evaluate this approach on Ethereum actor classification by comparing Transformer and BiLSTM sequence models against XGBoost and SVM baselines. We contribute a framework for leakage-aware Ethereum actor classification and a Transaction Grammar representation of wallet behavior. Our results demonstrate that, under leakage-aware evaluation, XGBoost outperforms Transformer-based sequence models while providing lower latency and estimated energy use.

</details>


### [98] [Compression-Based Behavioral Similarity for Open-World Sybil Discovery on Ethereum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27370)
*Michał Bartnicki,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于压缩相似性（Gzip-NCD）的Sybil攻击者检测框架，通过从EVM交易轨迹中提取符号化交易语法（Transaction Grammar），无需依赖钱包间的直接资金转账即可发现Sybil机器人，并针对时间漂移和对抗性扰动进行了鲁棒性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
