Furthermore, by introducing pattern matrices and generic rank, the almost-everywhere existence of this optimal basis in the parameter space is rigorously proved, perfectly aligning with the definition of strong structural controllability. To break the NP-hard combinatorial bottleneck of manually pre-defining the targets, a polynomial-time automated discovery algorithm based on the multi-layer Weisfeiler-Lehman (WL) color refinement is proposed. Finally, the strong structural observability and invariant attributes of the network are evaluated. Numerical examples with asymmetric matrix weights and directed multi-layer topologies are provided to verify the derived theorems.

</details>


### [197] [Data-Driven Formal Methods for Complex Dynamical Systems: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27908)
*Behrad Samari,Alessandro Abate,Antoine Girard,Majid Zamani,Amy Nejati,Abolfazl Lavaei*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文是一篇全面综述，系统性地梳理了数据驱动形式化方法在动力学系统验证与控制器综合中的研究进展，将文献按三大方法论支柱（有限/无限抽象、函数证书、组合方法）组织，并分类了数据驱动保证的三种类型（统计保证、Lipschitz连续性保证、结构性质保证），同时兼顾确定性与随机系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动形式化方法为缺乏精确模型的复杂动力学系统提供了带形式保证的验证与综合手段，但现有研究结果分散、缺乏统一组织，难以清晰理解其原理、区别和实际潜力，因此需要一篇综合性综述来填补这一空白。

Method: 该综述围绕形式化方法中的三大方法论支柱组织文献：(1)基于（不）有限抽象的技術；(2)函数证书方法（如控制屏障证书）；(3)组合方法。同时将数据驱动保证分为三类：基于PAC和场景框架的统计保证、基于Lipschitz连续性的保证、以及利用结构性质的保证。

Result: 综述发现确定性系统的文献远比随机系统丰富，并系统分类了已有方法；同时指出了随机系统与确定性系统在数据驱动验证与综合中存在的固有差异和挑战。

Conclusion: 本文为数据驱动形式化方法领域提供了一个系统性的组织框架，有助于研究者理解各类方法的原理与适用场景，并指出了随机系统方面尚需进一步探索的研究方向。

Abstract: Data-driven approaches with formal guarantees have recently emerged as a powerful means for the verification and controller synthesis of complex dynamical systems. Interest in these methods is rapidly growing, as system models are often unavailable in practice, and challenges such as nonlinear behavior, uncertainty, and the curse of dimensionality typically render accurate modeling infeasible. These difficulties motivate leveraging limited data collected from the system while still providing formal guarantees on its overall behavior. The community has therefore proposed a few hundred articles on the development of data-driven frameworks that enable the formal verification and synthesis of dynamical systems without explicit models, addressing complex specifications beyond stability. Despite this rapid growth, existing results remain scattered and lack a coherent organization, limiting a clear understanding of their principles, distinctions, and practical potential. This survey fills this gap by providing a comprehensive overview of these data-driven methods for both deterministic and stochastic dynamical systems. We structure the literature around three main methodological pillars in formal methods: (in)finite-abstraction-based techniques, functional certificate approaches, such as control barrier certificates, and compositional methods. For each of these approaches, we classify the resulting data-driven guarantees into three main categories: (i) statistical guarantees grounded in probably approximately correct and scenario-based frameworks, (ii) guarantees derived from Lipschitz continuity, and (iii) guarantees exploiting structural properties. While the literature on deterministic systems is considerably richer, we also devote particular attention to the stochastic counterpart, highlighting the inherent differences and challenges that arise compared to the deterministic case.

</details>


### [198] [Projection-Regularized Indirect Data-Driven Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28123)
*Mahmood Mazare,Hossein Ramezani*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出投影正则化预测控制（PRPC），通过解析凝聚的正则化投影保留下基本引理权重向量，并利用偏差-方差分析证明其在过程噪声和结构秩亏下优于无正则化子空间方法，进一步用于LTV系统的自适应滑窗控制，并通过向量值鞅集中不等式推导有限样本置信界，实现鲁棒递推可行性与ISpS稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间接数据驱动预测控制方法在过程噪声和数据稀缺情况下性能退化，缺乏严格的理论保证，尤其是在闭环数据相关性下的安全性保证。

Method: 1）提出PRPC，将正则化投影解析凝聚为固定维度的协方差更新；2）进行严格的偏差-方差分析证明预测误差严格降低；3）设计自适应滑窗控制器应对LTV系统；4）利用向量值鞅集中不等式推导均匀时间有限样本置信界；5）将统计不确定度半径嵌入动态收紧的约束集合，保证鲁棒递推可行性和ISpS。

Result: 在LTI和LTV基准上的仿真表明PRPC具有实时可计算性和严格的约束满足，且理论分析证明其在过程噪声和秩亏情形下预测误差严格低于无正则化方法。

Conclusion: PRPC为数据驱动预测控制提供了一种兼具理论保证（方差分析、有限样本置信界、ISpS稳定性）和实际计算效率的框架，尤其适用于噪声环境和时变系统。

Abstract: Indirect data-driven predictive control methods often suffer under process noise and data scarcity. This paper introduces Projection-Regularized Predictive Control (PRPC), retaining the fundamental-lemma weight vector via a regularized projection analytically condensed into an efficient, fixed-dimension covariance update. A rigorous bias--variance analysis proves PRPC strictly reduces prediction error under process noise (errors-in-variables) and structural rank deficiencies compared to unregularized subspace methods. We leverage these properties to develop an adaptive sliding-window controller for linear time-varying (LTV) systems. To guarantee safety despite closed-loop data correlations, we derive a uniform-in-time, finite-sample confidence bound on the empirical predictor using vector-valued martingale concentration inequalities. Embedding this statistical uncertainty radius into a dynamically tightened constraint set rigorously ensures robust recursive feasibility and Input-to-State practical Stability (ISpS) with high probability. Simulations on LTI and LTV benchmarks demonstrate real-time tractability and strict constraint satisfaction.

</details>


### [199] [Data-Driven Dead-Zone Compensation via Projection in Predictive Control Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28142)
*Mahmood Mazare,Hossein Ramezani*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无模型、无死区参数化的数据驱动架构，通过识别一个绝对子空间预测器作为数据驱动的稳态传感器，将执行器死区导致的预测残差作为代理嵌入行为Hankel矩阵中，将失配估计简化为单一固定正交投影，实现无需动态估计器、无探测信号、无在线预测误差计算的偏移自由跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行器死区是运动控制中常见且棘手的非线性问题，会导致稳态偏移或极限环。现有补偿方法通常需要植物模型或死区参数化，这在许多实际场景中难以获得，因此需要一种无需模型和死区参数化的数据驱动补偿方案。

Method: 在速度形式预测器之外，同时识别一个绝对子空间预测器。由于绝对预测器不含积分作用，其行为类似于数据驱动的稳态传感器，持续的执行器失配表现为比例预测残差。将该残差作为代理嵌入行为Hankel矩阵，将失配估计简化为单个固定的在线正交投影，无需动态估计器、注入探测信号或运行时的预测误差计算。该框架被集成到子空间预测控制器中。

Result: 框架被证明是递归可行且实际输入到状态稳定的，一旦死区穿越稳定后可恢复无偏移跟踪。在Quanser六阶轻度阻尼多自由度扭转系统上实时验证，在±0.18V执行器死区下实现无偏移跟踪；在高精度功率放大器上的第二项研究表明，该架构同样能抑制快速开关电力电子中的死区时间诱导非线性。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动架构在无需植物模型和死区参数化的前提下，能够有效补偿执行器死区非线性，实现无偏移跟踪，并具备理论保障（递归可行性和实际ISS稳定性），且在不同应用场景（机械系统和电力电子）中均验证了其通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Actuator dead-zones are a common and troublesome nonlinearity in motion control: a band of commanded effort over which the plant does not respond, leaving a steady-state offset or a limit cycle. This paper proposes a data-driven architecture that compensates such mismatches without a model of the plant and without any parameterization of the dead-zone. The central idea is to identify, alongside the velocity-form predictor used for control, a second absolute subspace predictor. Because the absolute predictor carries no integral action, it behaves as a data-driven steady-state sensor, so a persistent actuator mismatch appears as a proportional prediction residual. Embedding this residual as a proxy in a behavioral Hankel matrix reduces the mismatch estimate to a single fixed orthogonal projection evaluated online, with no dynamic estimator, no injected probing signal, and no run-time prediction-error computation. Integrated into a subspace predictive controller, the framework is shown to be recursively feasible and practically input-to-state stable, and it recovers offset-free tracking once the dead-band traversal settles. The approach is validated in real time on a sixth-order, lightly damped Quanser multi-DOF torsion system, whose complex-conjugate poles give a lightly damped open-loop response, achieving offset-free tracking across a $\pm 0.18$\,V actuator dead-band. A second study on a high-precision power amplifier shows that the same architecture rejects dead-time-induced nonlinearities in fast-switching power electronics.

</details>


### [200] [Stochastic Average Consensus Filtering and Distributed State Estimation for Boolean Control Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28158)
*Rong Yang,Chi Huang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对布尔控制网络（BCNs）提出了一种分布式多传感器融合状态估计与一致性滤波方法，通过结合概率测度变换、半张量积和随机逼近，设计了分布式随机平均一致性滤波器，并利用鞅收敛定理和摄动随机Lyapunov函数证明了算法的几乎必然收敛性，实现了通过局部通信完成全局状态估计，克服了集中式架构的高通信成本和单点故障问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集中式多传感器估计方案用于随机布尔控制网络时存在两个主要缺陷：一是通信成本高昂，二是存在单点故障风险；同时，连续状态的一致性算法难以直接扩展到离散逻辑系统中。

Method: 将概率测度变换、半张量积和随机逼近三种工具相整合，设计了一个分布式随机平均一致性滤波器。在理论上，利用鞅收敛定理和摄动随机Lyapunov函数来证明算法的几乎必然收敛性。

Result: 所提出的分布式框架能够通过局部通信实现全局状态估计，有效避免了集中式架构的缺陷，并且算法具有几乎必然收敛性保证。

Conclusion: 该研究为解决布尔控制网络的分布式多传感器融合估计问题提供了一种可行的新方案，弥合了连续状态一致性与离散逻辑系统之间的鸿沟，为实际应用提供了理论保障。

Abstract: This paper addresses the distributed multi-sensor fusion state estimation and consensus filtering for Boolean control networks (BCNs). Existing centralized multi-sensor estimation schemes for stochastic BCNs have limitations of high communication costs and single-point failures, and continuous-state consensus algorithms are difficult to extend to discrete logical systems. By integrating probability measure transformation, semi-tensor product and stochastic approximation, a distributed stochastic average consensus filter is proposed. Moreover, the almost sure convergence of the algorithm is proved by martingale convergence theorem and perturbed stochastic Lyapunov functions. The proposed framework realizes global state estimation via local communication, avoiding the defects of centralized architectures.

</details>


### [201] [Self-Evolving Learning for Embodied AI with Criticality Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28251)
*Linxuan He,Yuying Tian,Lingxiang Fan,Jiaqi Pi,Yinqiao Lu,Shang Su,Mengkai Shi,Shuo Feng*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自进化方法，通过学习策略自身执行结果来预测未来失败概率的状态关键性模型，指导重要性采样偏向容易失败的场景，从而突破具身AI在任务微调中的性能瓶颈。在四足运动、多任务操作、视觉-语言-动作基准和真实机器人任务上，相比基线降低51-67%的失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI系统在任务特定微调时经常陷入性能瓶颈。根本原因在于微调数据的收集方式：默认流程随机采集数据，将所有样本视为同等信息量，导致数据集被名义场景主导，而最具有改进价值的稀有失败案例却被遗漏。

Method: 提出一种自进化方法。核心思路是：从策略自身执行结果中学习一个状态关键性模型，用于预测未来失败的概率，并将其作为指导信号进行重要性采样，使采样偏向故障频发的场景。在用多样化故障场景替换冗余的名义场景后，使用重要性权重在训练期间对数据进行重采样，从而在保持无偏学习目标的同时，从根本上提高训练池的信息密度。

Result: 在四足运动、多任务操作、视觉-语言-动作基准以及真实机器人任务上，该方法相比已训练基线降低了51-67%的失败率，相比最先进的视觉-语言-动作模型降低了8-25%的失败率。

Conclusion: 通过基于策略自身执行结果的关键性建模与重要性采样，能够有效打破具身AI微调的性能瓶颈，显著提升任务成功率，且具备跨多种任务和真实场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: Despite rapid advances in policy pretraining, embodied AI systems routinely plateau during task-specific finetuning. The root cause lies in how finetuning data are collected: the default pipeline gathers data randomly, treating every sample as informative. Datasets become dominated by nominal scenarios, while rare failure cases--the most valuable for improvement--are missed. We propose a self-evolving method that breaks this plateau. Our core insight is that a state-wise criticality model, learned from the policy's own execution outcomes to predict the probability of future failure, can guide importance sampling toward failure-prone scenarios. After replacing redundant nominal scenarios with diverse failure-prone ones, importance weights are used to resample the data during training. This effectively preserves an unbiased learning objective while fundamentally increasing the information density of the training pool. Across quadrupedal locomotion, multi-task manipulation, vision-language-action benchmarks, and a real-robot task, our method reduces failure rates by 51--67% relative to trained baselines and by 8-25% relative to state-of-the-art vision-language-action models.

</details>


### [202] [Grid-Forming Converter DC-link Control Considering the Primary Energy Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28270)
*Carlo de Paolis Robles,Andrés Tomás-Martín,Ignacio Egido,Aurelio García-Cerrada*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对电网中可再生能源替代传统同步发电机导致的惯性降低问题，提出在电网形成型电压源换流器的直流母线电压控制设计中，明确考虑一次能源源的动态响应和运行限制，并采用遗传算法系统整定控制参数，以减少功率突变时换流器脱网风险，增强系统韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着传统同步发电机被换流器接口的可再生能源逐步替代，电力系统常规惯性降低，威胁系统稳定性。虽然已有大量文献研究电网形成型换流器的直流母线电压调节，但多数方法隐式假设理想且不受约束的直流电源，未对一次能源源的动态响应和运行限制（这些因素在暂态过程中会显著影响直流侧可用功率）进行建模或纳入直流母线控制设计，导致换流器可能因功率突变而脱网。

Method: 提出一种系统化的整定方法，在设计阶段将一次能源源的动态特性和运行限制纳入直流母线电压控制设计；采用基于遗传算法（Genetic Algorithm）的方法对控制参数进行系统整定；通过详细的电磁暂态（EMT）模型进行仿真验证所提控制的性能。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在设计阶段纳入一次能源源动态响应和运行限制，能够降低换流器在功率突变时从电网脱网的风险，并增强系统的韧性。

Conclusion: 将一次能源源的约束纳入电网形成型换流器的直流母线控制设计是可行且有益的策略，可提升系统在暂态扰动下的鲁棒性和持续运行能力。

Abstract: The gradual substitution of conventional synchronous generators by converter-interfaced renewable energy sources raises concerns about the reduction of conventional inertia in electric power systems and the ensuing threat to their stability. In this regard, grid-forming voltage source converters have been proposed as a key solution to address this challenge. Although a growing body of literature addresses DC-link voltage regulation in grid-forming converters, most existing approaches implicitly assume an ideal and unconstrained DC power source. As a result, the dynamic response and operational limits of the primary energy source, which can critically shape the available DC-side power during transients, are rarely modeled or explicitly accounted for in the design of the DC-link control. This paper demonstrates that incorporating these aspects at the design stage reduces the risk of converter disconnection from the power grid under sudden power imbalances, while enhancing system resilience. A systematic methodology based on a Genetic Algorithm is proposed to tune the control parameters. The performance of the proposed control is validated by simulation using a detailed electromagnetic transient model.

</details>


### [203] [Adaptive Demand-Driven Energy Management of PCM-Integrated District Heating Systems: Operational Flexibility and Techno-Economic Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28280)
*Xin Jin,Chunjun Huang,Pei Huang,Natasa Nord*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个结合热泵废热回收和自适应需求驱动（ADD）控制策略的相变材料（PCM）集成区域供热系统动态仿真模型，并与基线案例和基于规则的控制（RBC）方法进行对比。结果表明，ADD策略有效平滑了热需求曲线，实现了高达5.3%的峰值负荷削减，同时维持室内热舒适；灵敏度分析发现80°C相变温度和高于2 W/(m·K)的导热系数可获得更好的削峰和经济性能，但25.3年的投资回收期表明仍需进一步降低成本并引入市场激励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜热热能储存（LHTES）利用相变材料（PCM）是区域供热（DH）中转移供热和降低峰值需求的有前景方案，但PCM热物理特性与实际控制策略对DH系统层面运行和经济性能的综合影响尚未被充分理解。本研究旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 开发了PCM集成区域供热系统（含热泵辅助废热回收）的动态仿真模型，采用自适应需求驱动（ADD）控制策略，并与基线案例和基于规则的控制（RBC）方法进行比较。评估指标包括峰值负荷削减、运行成本、热泵性能和室内热舒适。此外，还进行了灵敏度分析以考察PCM热物理特性的影响。

Result: RBC策略可以转移峰值需求但会在充能期间产生次级峰值；ADD策略平滑了热需求曲线，实现高达5.3%的峰值负荷削减并保持热舒适。灵敏度分析显示，80°C相变温度和高于2 W/(m·K)的导热系数可获得更高的峰值削减和更好的经济性能。但系统回收期为25.3年，表明需要进一步成本降低和市场激励。

Conclusion: 所提出的ADD控制策略为增强区域供热灵活性提供了显著潜力，支持向未来低碳能源系统过渡；但仍需进一步降低成本并引入支持性市场激励措施以提升经济可行性。

Abstract: Latent heat thermal energy storage (LHTES) using phase change materials (PCMs) is a promising solution to shifting heat supply and reducing peak demand in district heating (DH). However, the combined impacts of PCM thermophysical properties and practical control strategies on DH system-level operational and economic performance remain insufficiently understood. To bridge the research gap, this study investigates a PCM-integrated DH system with heat pump assisted waste heat recovery under an adaptive demand-driven (ADD) control strategy to enhance operational flexibility. A dynamic simulation model was developed and the system performance was evaluated against a baseline case and a rule-based control (RBC) approach based on peak-load reduction, operational cost, heat pump performance, and indoor thermal comfort. Furthermore, sensitivity analyses were conducted to examine the influence of PCM thermophysical properties on system performance. The results showed that the RBC can shift peak demand but tends to generate secondary peaks during charging periods. In contrast, the ADD strategy effectively smoothed the heat demand profile and achieved up to 5.3% peak-load reduction while maintaining thermal comfort. Sensitivity analysis revealed that a phase-change temperature of 80$^\circ$C and thermal conductivity above 2 W/(m$\cdot$K) achieved a higher peak-load reduction and improved economic performance. Despite the enhanced peak-shaving capability achieved by the proposed control strategy, the system exhibited a payback period of 25.3 years, indicating that further cost reductions and supportive market incentives are required. Nevertheless, the proposed approach provides significant potential for enhancing DH flexibility and supporting the transition toward future low-carbon energy systems.

</details>


### [204] [Input-to-state Stable Approximate Nonlinear Model Predictive Control with Realtime Feasibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28353)
*Jan Olucak,Torbjørn Cunis*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算轻量级的近似鲁棒非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）方法，基于输入-状态控制李雅普诺夫函数和鲁棒控制屏障函数对，扩展了一种近期提出的标称无穷小时域NMPC方案，使非线性约束系统能在嵌入式硬件上实时求解（仅需小规模二次规划），并通过航天器控制数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒NMPC通常计算代价高，难以在嵌入式硬件上实时运行。需要在保证鲁棒性的前提下降低计算复杂度，使非线性约束系统的鲁棒控制能够在资源受限平台上实时实现。

Method: 基于输入-状态控制李雅普诺夫函数与鲁棒控制屏障函数对，构建一个计算轻量级的近似鲁棒NMPC控制律。该方法建立在近期提出的标称无穷小时域NMPC方案基础上，通过小规模二次规划来计算反馈控制律，从而降低计算负担。

Result: 在非线性约束航天器控制问题上进行了数值实验，并与文献中其他鲁棒NMPC方案进行了比较，验证了所提方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方案在保持鲁棒性的同时显著降低了计算复杂度，能够在嵌入式硬件上实时运行，为非线性约束系统的鲁棒控制提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, a computationally lightweight approximate robust nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) law is proposed based on a pair of input-to-state control Lyapunov function and robust control barrier function. The result builds upon and augments a recently introduced nominal infinitesimal- horizon NMPC scheme which permits small-sized quadratic programs to compute the feedback law for nonlinear constraint systems on embedded hardware in real time. Numerical experiments for nonlinear constrained spacecraft control and comparison to other robust NMPC schemes from the literature demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed scheme.

</details>


### [205] [Anonymous sharing is pairwise phase-blind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28377)
*Brieuc Le roux tardif*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了共享存储系统中检查点写入的"检查点风暴"现象，将自我强化建模为通过共享资源耦合的整合-发放振荡器群体，证明在该模型下相位锁定机制不成立。对于匿名资源（吞吐量仅取决于活跃用户数），双作业相位间隙映射为恒等映射，两体耦合完全缺失；匿名性还会冻结触发顺序，使同步态无法从外部到达。仅存的效应是三阶效应：重叠写入窗口的映射具有倒数谱和单位行列式，使同步成为具有扩张方向的固定点而非吸引子。数值实验表明随机启动的作业群既不锁相也不聚类，但并发写入的上尾仍高于独立相位值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检查点写入引发的突发性相关I/O被广泛描述为自我强化的"检查点风暴"，但缺乏严格的数学形式化验证。作者希望检验这一普遍认知是否正确——即共享存储系统中多个独立训练任务是否真的会因为检查点写入而产生相关的相位锁定和突发行为。

Method: 将检查点写入任务形式化为通过共享资源耦合的整合-发放振荡器群体（integrate-and-fire oscillators），分析匿名资源（吞吐量仅取决于活跃用户数而非具体用户）下的相位动力学。通过双作业返回映射、N作业区间映射和频谱分析，系统地研究了同步态的存在性、稳定性和可达性，并用数值实验验证理论结果。

Result: 主要发现：1) 匿名资源下两体耦合完全缺失（相位间隙映射为恒等）；2) 匿名性冻结触发顺序，任何轨迹都无法到达同步态；3) 仅存三阶效应：写窗口重叠时映射为a_j ↦ ((N-j)/j)a_j，具有倒数谱和单位行列式，同步是固定点但不是吸引子（有⌈N/2⌉-1个扩张方向）；4) 该结果来源于匿名性而非公平性（一般匿名吞吐量函数下谱积仍为1）；5) 数值实验显示无锁相但并发写入上尾仍超过独立相位值；6) 异构作业在功率上限约束下才出现真正的两体耦合。

Conclusion: "检查点风暴"作为自我强化的相位锁定现象在匿名资源共享模型下并不成立——两体耦合是缺失的而非弱的，同步状态是不可达的。但缺乏锁定并不意味着缺乏突发性：并发写入的尾部行为仍然超出独立基线。这一定性结论在异构作业和绑定功率上限的场景中不再成立，表明现象的真实机制比传统叙述更为微妙。

Abstract: Independent training jobs sharing a storage system write their checkpoints through the same finite bandwidth, and the resulting bursts of correlated I/O are commonly described as a self-reinforcing "checkpoint storm". We formalise the self-reinforcement as phase locking in a population of integrate-and-fire oscillators coupled through a shared resource, and show that within that model it fails. Call a resource anonymous if the rate it delivers to an active user depends on how many users are active and not on which. For identical jobs whose write is shorter than their compute interval, an anonymous resource produces no pairwise coupling at all: the two-job return map of the phase gap is the identity, under storage contention, under a shared power cap and under both, so the two-body interaction on which the Kuramoto and Mirollo-Strogatz frameworks are built is not weak here but absent. Anonymity also freezes the firing order, for any fleet size and any cap, so no trajectory reaches the synchronous state from outside it. What survives is a third-order effect: where all $N$ write windows overlap and the cap does not bind, the map is diagonal in the intervals between consecutive write starts, $a_j \mapsto ((N-j)/j)a_j$, with reciprocal spectrum and unit determinant, making synchrony a fixed point with $\lceil N/2\rceil-1$ expanding directions rather than an attractor. That determinant follows from anonymity and not from fairness: for any anonymous throughput $f$ with $f(n)\le n$ the spectrum becomes $(N-j)f(j)/(j f(N-j))$, whose product is still one. Numerically, a fleet launched at random neither locks nor clusters, and absence of locking is not absence of bursts: the upper tail of the number of concurrent writers stays above its independent-phase value. Heterogeneous jobs behind a binding cap do acquire a genuine pairwise coupling, which is where the statement stops generalising.

</details>


### [206] [Potentials and Limitations on Different Busbar Protections in Industrial Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28453)
*Karl M. H. Lai,Yunhe Hou,Kwunhang Wong*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了工业应用中各类母线保护（BBP）方案，包括漏电至框架、高阻抗差动（87Z）、联锁过流（ILOC）、电子（EBBP）和数字（NBBP）等方案，从工作原理、实施要求和适用母线结构等方面进行比较分析，并指出各方案在运行中的局限性及权衡，强调鲁棒母线保护设计对防止级联停电和维持系统可靠性的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2022年台湾"303大停电"事件由短路故障引发并因CT闭锁（supervision lockout）而加剧，暴露了集中式电网中母线保护设计的严重缺陷。尽管已有大量学术和工业指导，母线保护设计在CT安装位置、动态区域选择、CT饱和和不断演变的故障场景等方面仍面临实际挑战，且现有标准和指南未能充分反映多样系统条件下的运行限制。

Method: 对五种母线保护方案（漏电至框架、87Z高阻抗差动、ILOC联锁过流、EBBP电子式和NBBP数字式）进行结构化评估，从工作原理、实施要求和不同母线配置的适用性三个维度进行对比分析，同时考察NBBP中CT监督、检查区域和方向性检查的集成以减轻误动作。

Result: 比较分析揭示了不同母线保护方案之间存在明显的权衡关系。论文指出NBBP通过整合CT监督、检查区域和方向性检查能够有效减少误动作，同时系统性识别了各方案在不同运行条件下的操作局限性。

Conclusion: 坚固的母线保护设计在防止级联停电和维持系统可靠性方面发挥着关键作用。本文通过系统的方案比较，为工业应用中母线保护方案的选择和设计提供了参考依据。

Abstract: Busbar protection is a cornerstone of industrial power system reliability, as failures at switchgear can propagate rapidly and extend restoration times. The Taiwan "303 blackout" in 2022, initiated by a short-circuit fault and aggravated by CT supervision lockout, illustrates the severe consequences of inadequate designs on busbar protection in centralized grids. Despite extensive academic and industrial guidance, practical challenges remain in busbar protection design, including CT placement, dynamic zone selection, CT saturation, and evolving fault scenarios. Standards and guides often fail to reflect operational limitations under diverse system conditions. This paper provides a structured evaluation of busbar protection (BBP) schemes in industrial applications. Leakage-to-frame, high-impedance differential (87Z), interlocking overcurrent (ILOC), electronic (EBBP), and numerical (NBBP) schemes are examined with respect to their operating principles, implementation requirements, and suitability for different bus configurations. Comparative analysis highlights clear trade-offs in different busbar protection schemes. The main contributions are: (i) systematic review of classical and modern busbar protection schemes, (ii) identification of operational limitations with various busbar protection, and (iii) comparison on the integration of CT supervision, check zones, and directional checks in NBBP to mitigate maloperations. The discussion reinforces the role of robust busbar protection design in preventing cascading outages and maintaining system reliability.

</details>


### [207] [On the Implementation of Two-Parameter Stabilizing Controllers Using Stable-Block Input-Output Feedback Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28469)
*Fernando di Sciascio*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的实现方法，利用纯稳定构成模块来实现连续和离散时间二自由度（2-DOF）稳定化控制器。该方法适用于所有规则SISO和MIMO被控对象，尤其是那些不满足奇偶交错特性（PIP）而不可避免地需要不稳定控制器的系统。与现有代数方法不同，本文方法不牺牲参数设计自由度，通过Youla-Kučera参数化在输入输出（I/O）反馈拓扑中解耦综合与实现，并通过稳定模块的循环配置来实现控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代数方法为了强制子模块稳定而牺牲了参数设计自由度，这在处理不满足奇偶交错特性的被控对象时（此时不稳定控制器不可避免）存在局限。需要一个既能保持设计自由度又能用稳定模块实现控制器的通用框架。

Method: 利用Youla-Kučera参数化方法在输入输出（I/O）反馈拓扑结构中进行控制器综合，将物理性能指标驱动的控制器设计与具体实现解耦；随后通过一组稳定构成模块的循环配置架构来实现所设计的控制器。

Result: 该方法适用于所有规则SISO和MIMO被控对象（包括不满足奇偶交错特性的系统），并保留了完整的参数设计自由度。所提出的稳定模块循环配置架构带来了局部抗饱和、简化初始化和改善数值鲁棒性等工程优势。

Conclusion: 通过解耦综合与实现，本文提出的方法在不牺牲设计自由度的前提下，实现了用纯稳定模块构造任意所需控制器的目标，为不满足奇偶交错特性系统的控制提供了一种通用且具备工程实用性的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper a universal implementation method for continuous- and discrete-time two-parameter (2-DOF) stabilizing controllers using exclusively stable constituent blocks. The framework is valid for all regular SISO and MIMO plants, particularly those failing to satisfy the parity interlacing property where unstable controllers are unavoidable. Unlike existing algebraic approaches that compromise parametric design freedom to force sub-block stability, the proposed method preserves design precedence. By leveraging the Youla-Kučera parametrization within input-output (I/O) feedback topologies, we decouple synthesis from implementation. This allows physical performance specifications to drive the controller design, which is subsequently realized via a cyclic configuration of stable blocks. This architecture yields key engineering advantages, including localized anti-windup, simplified initialization, and improved numerical robustness.

</details>


### [208] [A subspace approach to data-driven predictive control for linear parameter-varying systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28490)
*Federico Porcari,Chris Verhoek,Valentina Breschi,Roland Tóth,Simone Formentin*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于线性参数变化（LPV）系统的子空间数据驱动预测控制方法。该方法从创新形式的仿射LPV状态空间模型出发，推导出能够分离过去数据、未来输入、调度轨迹和创新项影响的多步预测器，并通过投影到提升后的输入-输出-调度数据行空间，获得渐近无偏的数据驱动预测器。为使其可计算，引入了基于LQ分解的LPV扩展γ-DDPC公式，将在线决策变量数量固定为与数据集长度无关，并提出降阶预测器以抑制调度相关回归器的指数增长并放宽持续激励条件。仿真结果表明该方法具有良好跟踪性能、更强的抗噪鲁棒性和更低的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LPV系统的数据驱动预测控制（DDPC）方法在实际应用中面临计算成本高、对测量噪声敏感以及受持续激励条件限制等问题，难以在多步预测和较长过去时域下实际部署，因此需要一种更加高效、鲁棒且实用的LPV DDPC方案。

Method: 1) 从创新形式的仿射LPV状态空间模型推导多步预测器，分离过去数据、未来输入、调度轨迹和创新项的影响；2) 将预测器投影到提升的输入-输出-调度数据行空间，获得渐近无偏的数据驱动预测器；3) 引入基于LQ分解的LPV γ-DDPC扩展，固定在线决策变量数量与数据集长度无关；4) 提出降阶预测器以抑制调度相关回归器的指数增长并放宽持续激励条件。

Result: 仿真研究（包括不平衡圆盘示例）表明，所提出的控制器实现了良好的跟踪性能；与现有LPV DDPC方案相比，对测量噪声具有更强的鲁棒性，且计算成本更低，使得多步LPV DDPC在更长的过去时域下也能实际部署。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入LQ分解和降阶预测器，成功解决了LPV DDPC在实际部署中的计算复杂度和鲁棒性瓶颈，为LPV系统的高效数据驱动预测控制提供了切实可行的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a subspace data-driven predictive control method for linear parameter-varying (LPV) systems. Starting from an affine LPV state-space model in innovation form, we derive a multi-step predictor that separates the effects of past data, future inputs, scheduling trajectories, and innovations. By projecting this representation onto the row span of lifted input-output-scheduling data, we obtain an asymptotically unbiased data-driven predictor that can be embedded directly in a receding-horizon control problem, without explicitly identifying an LPV model. To make the resulting LPV data-driven predictive control (DDPC) formulation tractable, we introduce an LPV extension of $γ$-DDPC based on an LQ factorization. This formulation fixes the number of online decision variables independently of the length of the dataset. A reduced-order predictor is then proposed to curb the exponential growth of scheduling-dependent regressors, which also relaxes the persistence-of-excitation condition. Simulation studies, including an unbalanced-disk example, show that the proposed controller achieves good tracking performance and, compared to existing LPV DDPC schemes, achieves better robustness to measurement noise and reduced computational cost, making multi-step LPV DDPC practically deployable, even with longer past horizons.

</details>


### [209] [A Sub-6G Mixer First RXFE with LO Overlap Reduction and 1.08 dB NF Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28546)
*Anushka Tripathi,Alan Nelson,Abhishek Srivastava*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对sub-6 GHz应用优化的mixer-first接收机前端（RXFE），通过最小化本振（LO）脉冲重叠来缓解输入匹配和噪声系数（NF）的退化。采用TSMC 65-nm CMOS工艺实现，在1-5.6 GHz频率范围内实现了S11低于-10 dB、NF退化仅1.08 dB的性能，功耗不超过12.19 mA（1.2 V供电），有效面积302 μm × 209 μm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在mixer-first接收机前端设计中，LO脉冲重叠会导致输入匹配和噪声系数性能退化，这一问题在sub-6 GHz宽带应用中尤为突出。现有设计缺乏对这一问题的系统性分析和优化方法，因此需要提出一种设计方法来缓解LO重叠带来的性能下降。

Method: 提出了一种设计方法论来缓解LO重叠引起的输入匹配和NF退化，并使用基于电流模式逻辑（CML）的LO发生器来验证该方法的有效性。通过最小化LO脉冲重叠来优化RXFE的整体性能。

Result: 在TSMC 65-nm CMOS工艺下实现，1-5.6 GHz频段内S11低于-10 dB，NF退化仅为1.08 dB，功耗不超过12.19 mA（1.2 V供电），有源面积为302 μm × 209 μm。

Conclusion: 该设计方法论有效缓解了LO脉冲重叠对mixer-first接收机性能的影响，在宽带范围内实现了良好的输入匹配和低NF退化，验证了所提方法的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: This work presents a mixer-first receiver front-end (RXFE) optimized for sub-6 GHz applications with minimized local oscillator (LO) pulse overlap. A design methodology is proposed to mitigate LO overlap-induced degradation in input matching and noise figure (NF), validated using a current-mode logic (CML)-based LO generator. Implemented in TSMC 65-nm CMOS, the RXFE achieves an input return loss (S11) below -10 dB, an NF degradation of 1.08 dB, and consumes no more than 12.19 mA from a 1.2 V supply across the 1-5.6 GHz frequency range. The proposed RXFE occupies an active layout area of 302 um x 209 um.

</details>

### [210] [ZAPs: A Reward Attribution Framework for DeFi Ecosystems with Adversarial-Robust Scoring via Parallel Anomaly Ensemble Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27859)
*Girish G N,Ashutosh Sahoo,Ajay Bhat,Akshay SP,Gurukiran S,Parag Paul,Dhanashekar Kandaswamy*

Main category: q-fin.GN

TL;DR: 本文提出ZAPs，一个结合经济贡献评分与对抗鲁棒性的去中心化金融奖励归属框架，通过复合活动评分、两层加权机制和四层防御栈来抵御机器人和女巫攻击，在真实场景中显著降低了女巫分配并提升了高质量钱包参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeFi领域许多激励计划依赖原始交易量、交易数和钱包数作为奖励依据，容易被机器人和女巫操作（sybil operations）利用。现有方案缺乏对经济贡献的准确衡量，也缺少对抗博弈的鲁棒性设计，导致激励资源被恶意参与者大量攫取。

Method: ZAPs框架包含两大核心组件：(1) 经济贡献评分：采用协议特定百分位归一化的复合活动评分限制巨鲸主导，同时保留用户间区分度；两层加权机制（协议在领域内份额×领域在生态内份额）降低在小协议上耕作（farming）的收益；(2) 四层防御栈：交易级完整性检查、并行异常集成模型（单类重构模型+孤立森林，采用渐进式而非二元惩罚）、分配后行为记忆、基于图的Sybil聚类检测。

Result: 在1,073个标记恶意钱包、124,638笔交易上，异常集成模型达到0.923±0.013的ROC-AUC，优于单独重构模型的0.891±0.016。受控模拟显示对抗性奖励攫取减少30-90%，合法用户场景仅变化1-8%。实际活动中，Sybil分配减少56%，高质量钱包参与提升49%，抛售压力降低50%。

Conclusion: ZAPs通过经济贡献评分与多层级对抗防御的结合，有效缓解了DeFi激励系统中的Sybil攻击和奖励滥用问题，同时保持对合法用户的公平性和最小负面影响。

Abstract: Incentive programs are central to user acquisition in decentralized finance, but many reward systems rely on raw volume, transaction count, and wallet count, making them vulnerable to bots and sybil operations. We present ZAPs, a reward attribution framework that combines economic contribution scoring with adversarial robustness. A composite activity score uses protocol-specific percentile normalization to limit whale dominance while preserving differentiation among users. A two-layer weighting mechanism combines protocol share within sector and sector share within the ecosystem, which reduces the profitability of farming small protocols. We show that the maximum reward obtainable from any protocol is bounded by that protocol's global volume share.
  ZAPs also introduces a four-layer defense stack consisting of transaction-level integrity checks, a parallel anomaly ensemble, post-distribution behavioral memory, and graph-based sybil clustering. The anomaly ensemble combines a one-class reconstruction model with an isolation forest and applies graduated rather than binary penalties. On 1,073 labeled malicious wallets covering 124,638 transactions, the ensemble achieves 0.923 +/- 0.013 ROC-AUC, compared with 0.891 +/- 0.016 for the reconstruction model alone, when the isolation forest is trained on benign wallets. Training it on the pooled population reverses its polarity and removes the ensemble gain. Controlled simulations reduce adversarial reward capture by 30-90 percent while legitimate-user scenarios change by 1-8 percent. Live campaigns recorded a 56 percent reduction in sybil allocation, a 49 percent increase in quality-wallet participation, and a 50 percent reduction in sell pressure.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [More Data, Worse Decisions? Preference Reversals in Neural Networks under Gram Incompatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27255)
*Yanli Yan,Yuanzheng Li,Yong Zhao,Hongbo Guo,Shoudong Han*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经网络在合并多源数据后是否保持基于案例决策理论（CBDT）组合公理所要求的决策偏好顺序，提出了精确与近似保持条件、尺度不变的Gram不匹配度量、几何导向正则化以及三阶段审计框架，并在负载出价代理、医疗和金融决策代理上验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络越来越多地跨群体、跨时间段和跨运行条件合并数据以提升泛化性能，但这引发了一个可靠性问题：在合并数据上重新训练的模型是否能保持各数据源支持的决策行动排序不变。CBDT的组合公理形式化了这一要求，但现有研究缺乏对该属性在固定表示神经网络中是否成立的分析，也缺乏可操作的测量与调控手段。

Method: 1）证明池化重新拟合会重计算用于加权源证据的逆Gram几何，这可能导致共享偏好反转，并推导出精确和近似保持条件；2）引入尺度不变的Gram不匹配度量用于候选池优先级排序，以及几何导向正则化用于在训练中塑造源几何；3）开发三阶段审计，通过决策变化追溯严格配对反转到任务定义的效用损失；4）在负载出价代理、医疗和金融决策代理上进行实验验证。

Result: 实验显示存在稳定和易反转的池化机制：负载审计在代理效用下识别出可测量的非零类别源共识相对有害决策；跨域审计显示相当的不匹配程度可能对应截然不同的保持率；几何导向目标占据不同的描述准确性-一致性-几何-危害操作点。

Conclusion: 该框架通过筛查、解析认证、几何导向训练和决策后果审计，使组合可靠性变得可测量且可操作，为跨源神经网络的决策可靠性保障提供了系统化的理论与工具支撑。

Abstract: Neural networks increasingly combine data across populations, time periods, and operating conditions to improve generalization. This raises a reliability question: whether a model refitted on pooled data preserves an action ordering supported by both sources. Case-Based Decision Theory (CBDT) formalizes this requirement through its composition axiom, which requires source-supported preferences to survive their union. We study when this property holds for fixed-representation neural networks with ordinary least squares (OLS) output heads. First, we show that pooled refitting recomputes the inverse-Gram geometry used to weight source evidence, which can reverse shared preferences, and derive exact and approximate preservation conditions. Next, we introduce a scale-invariant Gram mismatch measure for prioritizing candidate pools and geometry-oriented regularization for shaping source geometry during training. Finally, we develop a three-stage audit that traces strict pairwise reversals through decision changes to task-defined utility loss. Experiments spanning a load-based bidding proxy and medical and financial decision proxies reveal stable and reversal-prone pooling regimes: the load audit identifies a measurable nonzero class of source-consensus-relative harmful decisions under the proxy utility, while cross-domain audits show that comparable mismatch can correspond to sharply different preservation rates. Geometry-oriented objectives occupy distinct descriptive accuracy-consistency-geometry-harm operating points. Together, the framework makes compositional reliability measurable and operational through screening, analytic certification, geometry-oriented training, and decision-consequence auditing.

</details>


### [212] [Expected Survival-Time Bounds for Robust Optimization Over Time under Isotropic Gaussian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27280)
*Pavel Novoa-Hernández*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了鲁棒优化（ROOT）范式下，固定部署解在各项同性高斯环境动态中的期望生存时间。通过将生存建模为离散首出问题，推导出严格下界和可计算的多步上界，发现缓慢变化环境中期望生存时间以 Θ(σ⁻²) 规模增长，高维下趋于最小值1。蒙特卡洛仿真验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROOT是演化动态优化的新兴分支，重视解的持久性而非传统的追踪移动最优。虽然该领域发展迅速，但大多数贡献停留在算法和实证层面，生存时间这一衡量时间鲁棒性的关键指标在理论上缺乏深入理解——其期望值如何依赖于环境动态、部署质量和问题特征尚不清楚。

Method: 将生存时间的期望计算建模为离散首出问题（discrete first-exit problem），在各项同性高斯环境动态假设下，推导出期望生存时间的严格下界和可计算的多步上界；并通过蒙特卡洛仿真验证理论结果，检验对建模假设和参数不确定性的敏感性。

Result: 分析表明：缓慢变化环境中期望生存时间以 Θ(σ⁻²) 的量级增长；在高维情况下期望生存时间趋近于最小值（即仅能存活一个未来变化）。蒙特卡洛仿真验证了理论预测，并提供了一套可支持优化后部署决策的分析框架。

Conclusion: 该框架为部署寿命提供了解析刻画，能够判定给定部署时域是否可保证、可排除，或仍处于解析上不可解决的状态，从而为ROOT方法提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Robust Optimization Over Time (ROOT) is a recent branch of evolutionary dynamic optimization that seeks solutions capable of remaining effective across multiple consecutive environments. Unlike the traditional track-the-moving-optimum (TMO) paradigm, which reoptimizes after every environmental change, ROOT explicitly values persistence. Although the field has grown considerably, most contributions remain algorithmic and empirical, leaving several fundamental properties poorly understood from a theoretical perspective. One such property is survival time, defined as the number of future environments in which a deployed solution continues to satisfy a prescribed quality threshold. While survival time is widely used as a measure of temporal robustness, little is known about how its expected value depends on environmental dynamics, deployment quality, or problem characteristics. This paper studies expected survival time for a fixed deployed solution under isotropic Gaussian environmental dynamics. Modeling survival as a discrete first-exit problem, we derive a rigorous lower bound and a computable multi-step upper bound. The analysis shows that expected survival scales as $Θ(σ^-{2})$ in slowly varying environments and approaches its minimum value of one future change in high dimensions. A comprehensive Monte Carlo study validates the theoretical predictions, examines sensitivity to modeling assumptions and parameter uncertainty, and illustrates how the bounds can support deployment decisions after optimization. The resulting framework provides an analytical characterization of deployment lifetime and identifies when a required deployment horizon can be guaranteed, ruled out, or remains analytically unresolved.

</details>


### [213] [An analysis of binary isotonic regression: degrees of freedom and implications for calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27301)
*Raphael Rossellini,Rina Foygel Barber,Zhimei Ren,Jake A. Soloff*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对二元样本上保序回归（Isotonic Regression）的最坏情况自由度给出了完全精确的有限样本刻画，利用解析数论得到了首项为 $\frac{3}{(4\pi^2)^{1/3}} n^{2/3}$ 的锐利上界，并将其应用于校准任务，首次推导出保序回归期望校准误差（ECE）的非平凡无分布保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保序回归是估计单调函数和校准概率预测器的经典工具，但其最坏情况下的自由度（即拟合值数量）缺乏精确的有限样本刻画。同时，校准是概率预测的核心要求，而现有工作缺乏对保序回归期望校准误差的分布无关保证。

Method: 作者识别出能使保序回归产生最多不同拟合值的二元序列，利用解析数论技术推导出自由度的锐利上界（首项系数为 $\frac{3}{(4\pi^2)^{1/3}}$），并基于此确定性自由度界推导期望校准误差（ECE）的分布无关界，仅假设 $Y \in \{0,1\}$。

Result: 获得了保序回归在最坏情况下自由度的完全锐利的有限样本刻画，改进了先前上界；并首次得到保序回归 ECE 的非平凡、完全模型无关且分布无关的保证。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅深化了对保序回归统计复杂度的理论理解，还为概率预测校准提供了首个无分布保证，具有重要的理论与应用价值。

Abstract: Isotonic regression is a canonical tool for estimating monotone functions and calibrating probabilistic predictors. We provide a fully sharp finite-sample characterization of its worst-case degrees of freedom on binary samples. Specifically, we identify the binary sequences that maximize the number of distinct fitted values produced by isotonic regression. We develop a sharp bound on the degrees of freedom with a leading term of $\frac{3}{(4π^2)^{1/3}} n^{2/3}$ using analytic number theory, improving on previous bounds.
  We then apply this result to calibration. Calibration is a central requirement for probabilistic prediction, and isotonic regression is a widely used post-processing method for improving calibration. Building on deterministic degrees-of-freedom bounds, we derive, to our knowledge, the first nontrivial distribution-free guarantee on the Expected Calibration Error (ECE) of isotonic regression. This ECE bound is fully model-free and distribution-free, only assuming $Y \in \{0,1\}$.

</details>


### [214] [HOMER: Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation in Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27532)
*Kisung You,Boram Cho*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出HOMER（Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation），通过径向Huber中心聚合分块均值，在重尾数据下兼顾鲁棒性与均值效率。规范形式在二次区域内恢复样本均值，伪Huber形式随阈值增大趋近样本均值，并支持渐近线性推断与协方差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统重尾鲁棒估计存在两难：重尾分布削弱高置信度控制下经验均值的有效性，而几何中位数MOM缺乏向均值效率靠拢的阈值机制。需要一种能插值于中位数鲁棒性与经验均值效率之间的估计方法。

Method: 提出HOMER框架，将数据划分为多个块，计算各块均值后通过径向Huber中心聚合。提供规范Huber形式与伪Huber形式两种变形，两者均对每个分块分数有界，可实现鲁棒性与效率间的连续插值。

Result: 建立了Hilbert空间多数定理和有限二阶矩下的MOM阶偏离界。规范HOMER在二次区域内恢复样本均值；伪HOMER随阈值增大趋近样本均值，且具有渐近线性性和一致的sandwich协方差估计。有限三阶矩下，固定有限维投影以通常参数速率支持均值推断（需分块数和块大小同时增长且块大小更快）。仿真显示在少数分块汇总被扰动时保持稳定，干净高斯数据下接近经验均值效率；但有限块sandwich区间覆盖不足（尤其在偏斜函数型数据中），且当污染影响大多数块或破坏块内均值时方法失效。

Conclusion: HOMER通过径向Huber聚合提供了从MOM鲁棒性到均值效率的可调插值框架，理论性质良好，但仍存在有限块推断覆盖不足及极端污染场景下的失效局限，需进一步改进。

Abstract: Heavy tails weaken high-confidence control for the empirical mean. Geometric median-of-means (MOM) also lacks a threshold that moves toward mean efficiency. We propose \emph{HOMER}, or Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation. HOMER aggregates block means through a radial Huber center. Its canonical and pseudo-Huber forms bound each block score and interpolate between median-like robustness and the empirical mean. We establish a Hilbert-space majority theorem and a MOM-order deviation bound under a finite second moment. Canonical HOMER recovers the sample mean inside its quadratic region. Pseudo-HOMER approaches the sample mean as the threshold grows. It also admits asymptotic linearity and consistent sandwich covariance estimation around the population block-Huber target. Under a finite third moment, fixed finite-dimensional projections support mean inference at the usual parametric rate. This result requires growing block sizes and counts, with block sizes increasing faster. Heavy-tailed simulations show that HOMER remains stable when a minority of block summaries is displaced. On clean Gaussian data, both versions closely approach the empirical mean's efficiency. Finite-block sandwich intervals undercovered, especially for skewed functional data. Further studies show failure when contamination affects most blocks or compromises ordinary within-block means.

</details>


### [215] [Robust Wavelength Selection for Partial Least Squares Sugar Content Estimation Using Combinatorial Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27645)
*Mitsunobu Kanebako,Ami S. Koshikawa,Masaru Hitomi,Takuro Tanaka,Mahito Chiba,Maiko Mori,Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将近红外光谱波长区域选择建模为二元黑箱优化问题，并提出基于贝叶斯优化的方法，通过稀疏二次代理模型和Thompson采样结合模拟/量子退火求解，实验表明该方法优于遗传算法和模拟退火，且所选波长区域具有局部稳定性，不易过拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近红外光谱中波长选择对提高预测精度和可解释性至关重要，但现有方法（如遗传算法、模拟退火）选择的波长区域不稳定，容易陷入局部最优或过拟合解。

Method: 将波长区域选择形式化为二元黑箱优化问题，构建稀疏二次代理模型，通过Thompson采样顺序提取感兴趣波长区域，并将采集函数最小化转化为二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）问题，使用模拟退火或量子退火求解。

Result: 该方法提高了偏最小二乘回归的预测精度，比遗传算法和模拟退火产生更一致的波长区域。在一位局部扰动下，所选波长区域的验证集均方根误差波动极小，表明收敛到更平滑的误差景观。

Conclusion: 组合贝叶斯优化是光谱预测任务中鲁棒特征选择的有效框架，能够避免孤立的过拟合解。

Abstract: Wavelength selection is one of the important preprocessing methods in near-infrared spectroscopy to improve prediction accuracy and interpretability of spectral data. We formulate wavelength-region selection for sugar content estimation as a binary black-box optimization problem and propose a method based on Bayesian optimization. The proposed method constructs a sparse quadratic surrogate model and sequentially extracts interested wavelength regions by Thompson sampling. Minimizing an acquisition function is performed as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization problem by simulated or quantum annealing. Experiments show that the proposed method improves the prediction accuracy of partial least squares regression and yields more consistent wavelength regions than genetic-algorithm-based selection and simulated annealing. Under one-bit local perturbations, the selected wavelength regions show minimal fluctuations in root mean square errors between observed and predicted values of a validation set. This local stability suggests that our method converges to a smoother error landscape and avoids isolated overfitted solutions. These results indicate that combinatorial Bayesian optimization is a useful framework for robust feature selection in spectroscopic prediction tasks.

</details>


### [216] [Error Analysis of Neural-Network-Based Engression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27723)
*Juntong Chen,Zijian Guo,Xinwei Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对基于深度神经网络的Engression方法进行了理论误差分析，将超额风险分解为近似误差、随机误差和蒙特卡洛误差三个部分，并在目标条件生成器具有组合光滑结构的假设下建立了收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Engression方法通过能量分数（一种严格适当的评分规则）拟合生成模型来学习条件分布，但缺乏严格的理论误差分析。本文旨在为该方法的深度学习实现提供理论保障，填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将Engression方法的超额风险分解为三个组成部分：近似误差（approximation error）、随机误差（stochastic error）和蒙特卡洛误差（Monte Carlo error）。基于这一分解，在目标条件生成器具有组合光滑性结构的假设下推导收敛速率。

Result: 建立了基于深度神经网络实现Engression方法的收敛速率，该速率依赖于条件生成器的组合光滑结构。

Conclusion: 通过系统的误差分解分析，为Engression方法在深度学习框架下提供了理论误差保障和收敛速率保证。

Abstract: Engression (Shen and Meinshausen, 2024) learns a conditional distribution by fitting a generative model $Y = f(X,\varepsilon)$ under the energy score, a strictly proper scoring rule. We provide a theoretical error analysis of engression implemented with deep neural networks. We decompose the excess risk into three components: the approximation error, the stochastic error, and the Monte Carlo error. Based on this decomposition, we establish convergence rates under the assumption that the target conditional generator admits a compositional smoothness structure.

</details>


### [217] [Robust Estimation of Sparse Numerical Vectors under Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27815)
*Puning Zhao,Zhikun Zhang,Shaowei Wang,Sheng Yue,Bangzhou Xin,Tianhang Zheng,Pengfei Zhang,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出RPC（Randomized Projection with Clipping）方法，解决多项目用户场景下本地差分隐私（LDP）协议的投毒攻击防御问题。该方法通过服务器向用户发送随机二进制向量、用户对投影值进行裁剪来限制攻击者能力，并提出基于精确偏差分析的修正方法，消除了偏差-方差权衡，进一步缩小输出空间以增强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LDP防御策略仅针对单项目用户有效，而实际场景中用户可能拥有多个项目。多项目用户面临更大的输出空间，攻击者可以实施更强大的投毒攻击而不被发现，这使得防御变得极具挑战性。

Method: 提出RPC方法：1）服务器向每个用户发送随机二进制向量；2）用户将本地数据投影到该向量上并进行裁剪，以限制攻击者的能力；3）针对裁剪引入的偏差，提出基于精确偏差表达式分析的修正方法，从而无需在偏差和方差之间做权衡；4）裁剪阈值可以进一步减小，从而缩小输出空间并增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在可信环境下，新方法的性能与现有方法相当或更优，表明其本身就是一个高效的估计器。在不可信环境下，该方法对投毒攻击的鲁棒性显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RPC方法通过随机投影与裁剪机制结合精确偏差修正，有效解决了多项目用户场景下LDP的投毒攻击防御问题，在保证估计精度的同时显著增强了鲁棒性，并提供了严格的对抗所有可能攻击的理论保证。

Abstract: Local differential privacy (LDP) protocols are vulnerable to poisoning attacks. Existing research have proposed efficient defense strategies for single-item users. However, in practice, a user may possess multiple items. The defense against poisoning attacks for multi-item users is challenging, because due to larger output spaces, the adversary can conduct more powerful attacks without being detected. In this paper, we address the robust sparse vector mean estimation problem, in which each user has a vector with $m$ nonzero coordinates. We propose Randomized Projection with Clipping (RPC). Firstly, the server sends a random binary vector to each user. The user then projects its local data on the vector, and clip the value to restrict the attacker's capability. To handle clipping bias, we propose a correction method based on a careful analysis that gives an exact expression of the bias. As a result, bias-variance tradeoff is no longer needed, thus the clipping threshold can be further reduced to shrink the output space and enhance robustness. We provide a rigorous theoretical guarantee of the estimation error under all possible attacks. Numerical experiments show that under trusted environments, our new method achieves comparable or better performance than existing methods, indicating that our method is already an efficient estimator in its own right. Under untrusted environments, our method is also significantly more robust to poisoning attacks.

</details>


### [218] [Generalization and Trade-off in Adversarial Training: An RKHS Perspective via Kernel Integral Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27995)
*Yiling Xie,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）框架下研究对抗训练，推导了泛化误差界，发现对抗训练相比标准预测存在统计精度损失（源于鲁棒性与观测噪声的交互），并提出两阶段噪声去偏过程以恢复最优极小化收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练是应对对抗攻击的主流方法，但其在非参数框架下的泛化行为尚未被充分理解。特别是对抗鲁棒性与统计精度之间的内在权衡缺乏理论刻画，因此需要在RKHS框架下系统分析对抗训练估计器的泛化误差及其极限。

Method: 1) 通过核积分算子推导源均匀（source-uniform）泛化误差上界，刻画与鲁棒水平、样本量、源光滑度和核谱的关系；2) 在多项式谱模型下建立匹配的下界，证明最优平衡泛化速率可能慢于极小化预测基准；3) 提出两阶段噪声去偏（noise-debiased）估计器，先估计混合鲁棒项中的噪声贡献再将其去除，从而恢复最优速率。

Result: 揭示了对抗训练存在统计精度损失，该损失源于对抗鲁棒性与观测噪声的交互作用（噪声拖慢逼近速率但降低估计复杂度）；提出的去偏估计器在鲁棒水平按样本依赖阶数选取时可达极小化多项式速率（至多差对数因子），数值实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 本文刻画了非参数框架下对抗训练的泛化行为，为对抗鲁棒性与泛化之间的权衡提供了新解释和原则性解决方案，表明通过噪声去偏可以有效弥补对抗训练带来的统计精度损失。

Abstract: Adversarial training has emerged as a powerful approach for protecting models against adversarial attacks in a broad range of real-world applications. In this paper, we study adversarial training in the reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) framework through the associated kernel integral operator. We first derive source-uniform generalization error bounds for the RKHS adversarial training estimator in terms of the robustness level, sample size, source smoothness, and kernel spectrum. On a fixed polynomial-spectrum model, we further establish a matching lower bound showing that the optimally balanced generalization rate can be slower than the minimax prediction benchmark. This result reveals a loss of statistical accuracy in adversarial training. Our analysis shows that this loss arises from the interaction between adversarial robustness and observation noise: the noise contribution in the mixed robustness term slows the approximation rate, although the same term reduces the estimation complexity. To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage noise-debiased procedure that estimates and removes the noise contribution from the mixed term. The resulting estimator improves the generalization rate and attains the minimax polynomial rate, up to a logarithmic factor, when the robustness level is selected at the stated sample-dependent order. Our results characterize the generalization behavior of adversarial training in a nonparametric framework and provide a new interpretation and a principled solution for the trade-off between adversarial robustness and generalization. Numerical experiments support the theoretical findings and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [219] [On a joint simultaneous learning of relevant feature subsets and subspaces in regression-like problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28080)
*Illia Horenko*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文扩展了熵最优流形聚类（EOMC）方法，提出熵最优流形回归（EOMR），用于非平稳非线性回归问题中同时实现子集和子空间的联合识别。该方法具有线性迭代和内存复杂度，在Lorenz-96混沌系统和Hasegawa-Wakatani托卡马克等离子体模型上，相较于梯度提升随机森林、深度神经网络和TabPFN等前沿AI/ML工具，预测误差和模型复杂度均优于数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习工具在处理高维、非平稳、非线性的混沌和等离子体动力学问题时面临准确性和模型复杂度两方面的挑战，需要一种既能识别相关特征子集和子空间、又能保持低计算复杂度和高预测精度的方法。

Method: 将熵最优流形聚类（EOMC）扩展为熵最优流形回归（EOMR），能够在非平稳非线性回归问题中联合同时识别相关特征子集和子空间。该方法具有线性的迭代和内存复杂度，保证了学习的鲁棒性。

Result: 在Lorenz-96系统（F=8和F=12强混沌/极强混沌状态）和Hasegawa-Wakatani托卡马克边缘等离子体模型上，EOMR显著优于梯度提升随机森林、深度神经网络及TabPFN等基线方法，均方根预测误差和模型复杂度均优于数个数量级。对Hasegawa-Wakatani案例，EOMR仅用8个参数的线性因果弱平稳自回归过程即可精确描述主导EOF动力学。

Conclusion: EOMR在混沌和等离子体动力学等极具挑战性的问题上展现出卓越的预测能力和极低的模型复杂度，表明熵最优方法为高维非线性回归问题提供了一条高效且可解释的路径，显著优于现有主流AI和ML工具。

Abstract: We extend a recently introduced Entropy-Optimal Manifold Clustering (EOMC) to allow for a joint simultaneous identification of subsets and subspaces of relevant features in nonstationary and nonlinear regression problems. It is shown that the proposed extension - that we coin as Entropy-Optimal Manifold Regression (EOMR) - allows a robust learning with linearly-scaling iteration and memory complexities. EOMR is compared to the most complete set of state-of-the-art tools from the Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) that is available to the author, on the very challenging problems from chaotic and fluid dynamics: (i) on predicting the Lorenz-96 systems dynamics in strongly- and very-strongly chaotic regimes (with forcing parameter being $F=8$ and $F=12$, respectively); and, (ii) on a data from the Hasegawa-Wakatani model on the edge of the tokamak plasma. It is demonstrated that the proposed benchmarks (i) and (ii), indeed, are the very challenging problems for the state of the art ML and AI tools - since both the general-purpose gradient boosted random forests and deep neuronal networks, as well as transformer-based AI tools like TabPFN v.03 (more spezialised for large-dimensional small data learning problems) - result in orders of magnitude inferior root mean squared prediction errors, and orders of magnitude larger model complexities, when compared to the EOMR. For a Hasegawa-Wakatani example, EOMR distills a very simple entropy-optimal and skilful description of the leading Essential Orthogonal Function (EOF) dynamics, given by linear, causal and weakly-stationary autoregressive process described by just 8 parameters.

</details>


### [220] [Uncertainty quantification for trustworthy deep learning: Methods and measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28248)
*H. Martin Gillis,Thomas Trappenberg*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于深度学习不确定性量化（UQ）的结构化综述，聚焦于集成方法和近似贝叶斯方法，将生成预测分布的方法与总结不确定性的度量分离，系统地组织了五类方法家族，并统一了评估方法论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在安全关键领域的部署需要可靠的预测置信度估计，但传统架构缺乏原理性的不确定性量化机制。现有UQ综述缺乏对高效集成近似和单次通过方法的深入覆盖，需要统一的处理框架。

Method: 将UQ方法组织为五个家族：贝叶斯神经网络、蒙特卡洛Dropout、深度集成、高效集成近似、最后一层/单次通过方法；同时覆盖证据网络、先验网络、共形预测及事后校准等邻近工作；审查集成多样性理论与不确定性度量及分解，对比熵分解与成对散度度量；统一评估方法论，并以大语言模型中的不确定性作为延伸讨论。

Result: 提供了五个方法家族的深入评论，涵盖理论动机、实现、经验性能与局限性；结构化整理了多样性理论和不确定性度量（熵分解 vs 成对散度），并建立了共享的定性比较评估基础。

Conclusion: 通过分离"生成预测分布的方法"与"总结不确定性的度量"，提供了清晰的UQ全景图；指出了开放研究方向，包括分类的高效认知度量、最后一层多样性、分布偏移下的多样性与校准，以及混合架构。

Abstract: The deployment of deep neural networks in safety-critical domains demands reliable estimates of predictive confidence, yet conventional architectures lack principled uncertainty quantification. This survey provides a structured, critical review of methods for Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) in deep learning, scoped to ensemble-based and approximate Bayesian approaches and the measures used to summarize their outputs. Relative to existing UQ surveys, our contribution is depth on efficient ensemble approximations and single-pass methods, and a unified treatment that separates the method producing a predictive distribution from the measure that summarizes its uncertainty. We organize methods into five families: Bayesian neural networks, Monte Carlo Dropout, deep ensembles, efficient ensemble approximations, and last-layer or single-pass approaches. We situate adjacent work on evidential and prior networks, conformal prediction, and post-hoc calibration, together with the decision-time tasks of out-of-distribution detection and selective prediction. For each, we examine theoretical motivation, implementation, empirical performance, and limitations. We then review ensemble diversity theory and uncertainty measures and their decompositions, contrasting the entropy decomposition with pairwise divergence measures, and consolidate evaluation methodology so that our qualitative comparisons share a common basis. We close with a brief treatment of uncertainty in large language models and open research directions, including efficient epistemic measures for classification, last-layer diversity, diversity and calibration under shift, and hybrid architectures.

</details>


### [221] [Doubly Robust Functional Representation Learning for Longitudinal Causal Inference with Irregular Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28567)
*Mengfei Ran,Yifeng Shen,Ruijie Guan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出DR-FRL（双重稳健函数表示学习），一种交叉拟合工作流，将不规则历史数据（如实验室值、生理信号的函数型碎片）转化为目标估计量定向的状态表示，用于观察历史机制下的因果推断。该方法通过函数和时间编码器将点云与既往历史映射为状态，配合nuisance头估计结局、治疗和删失函数，并借助EIF定向的诊断工具进行验证。理论上证明在显式条件下估计量渐近线性，误差进入二阶乘积余项。模拟显示在高维函数混杂、信息性测量、弱支撑或重尾伪结局场景下有增益；VitalDB审计显示对于ICU处置终点，标量实验室汇总已包含大部分信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向因果研究常将历史记录为不规则函数碎片（不等时间间隔的实验室值、生理信号等），但标准双重稳健估计量通常需要标量汇总，而序列学习器优化的预测损失不一定能稳定有效影响函数（EIF）。因此需要一种能将不规则历史转化为EIF定向状态表示的估计框架。

Method: DR-FRL采用交叉拟合工作流：(1) 函数编码器和时间编码器将点云和先前历史映射为状态；(2) nuisance头估计结局、治疗和删失函数；(3) EIF定向的验证、校准、重叠、尾部与消融诊断评估状态是否支持估计方程；(4) 若状态保留EIF所需的nuisance信息，表示误差进入与普通nuisance误差相同的二阶乘积余项，估计量在显式速率、重叠、校准和稳定性条件下渐近线性；(5) Catoni聚合作为有界影响点估计单独处理，而非替代Wald推断。

Result: 模拟实验表明在高维函数混杂、信息性测量、弱支撑或伪结局重尾情况下DR-FRL优于基线。VitalDB审计显示DR-FRL能利用不规则的实验室点云进行分析，并得出有用的阴性结论：对于该ICU处置终点，标量实验室汇总已经携带了大部分与终点相关的信息。

Conclusion: DR-FRL为不规则纵向历史提供了EIF目标定向的表征学习框架，兼具双重稳健性质与函数型数据建模能力；同时通过实证审计展示了其在真实场景中的价值，包括识别出标量汇总在特定任务中可能已足够的情况。

Abstract: Longitudinal causal studies often record histories as irregular functional fragments: laboratory values, physiologic signals, sensor streams, and image-derived summaries measured at unequal and informative times. Standard doubly robust estimators usually require scalar summaries, whereas sequence learners optimize prediction losses that need not stabilize the efficient influence function. We propose Doubly Robust Functional Representation Learning (DR-FRL), a cross-fitted workflow that turns irregular histories into estimand-targeted states for observed-history regimes. Functional and temporal encoders map point clouds and prior histories into states; nuisance heads estimate outcome, treatment, and censoring functions; and EIF-targeted validation, calibration, overlap, tail, and ablation diagnostics assess whether the state supports the estimating equation. If the selected state preserves the nuisance information needed by the EIF, representation error enters the same second-order product remainder as ordinary nuisance error, and the mean estimator is asymptotically linear under explicit rate, overlap, calibration, and stability conditions. Catoni aggregation is treated separately as a bounded-influence point estimator, not a replacement for Wald inference. Simulations show gains when functional confounding is high-dimensional, measurement is informative, support is weak, or pseudo-outcomes are heavy-tailed. A VitalDB audit shows that DR-FRL can use irregular laboratory point clouds and deliver a useful negative finding: for this ICU-disposition endpoint, scalar laboratory summaries already carry much endpoint-relevant information.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [Entropy-Smooth Convex Optimization Cannot Be Accelerated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27476)
*Jacob M. Aguirre,Dmitrii M. Ostrovskii*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在负熵相对光滑的凸函数类中，任何一阶方法在单纯形上的收敛率下界为Ω(L/T)，表明镜像下降在此类问题中是最优的（直至对数因子）。该结果扩展到量子设置下的谱面体（Hermitian矩阵），证明von Neumann熵相对光滑函数类中同样存在非加速下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ℓ1范数光滑性假设下，加速方法（如加速镜像下降）是可行的，但Dragomir等人已证明在相对光滑性假设下加速可能不可能——然而他们的反例使用的是病态的、与困难实例共同构造的prox函数。本文旨在证明对于一个结构特别良好、自然的prox函数（负熵），加速同样不可能，从而填补这一空白，并延伸至量子计算场景。

Method: 作者构造了一个硬实例（hard instance），在标准的d维单纯形上，针对负熵相对光滑的凸函数类，证明了任何一阶方法的收敛率下界为Ω(L/T)，其中d=Ω(T²)。该下界通过信息论/对抗性论证建立，并将经典结果推广到量子设置，在谱面体（d×d Hermitian正半定且迹为1的矩阵）上针对von Neumann熵相对光滑的函数类证明相同的下界。

Result: ①在标准单纯形上，对于负熵相对光滑的凸函数类，任何一阶方法的收敛率下界为Ω(L/T)。②由此证明镜像下降（mirror descent）在此类问题中是最优的（相差对数因子）。③即使对于具有良好结构的prox函数（负熵），加速仍然不可能。④该下界被成功推广到量子设置（谱面体上的von Neumann熵相对光滑函数类）。

Conclusion: 本文表明，在相对光滑性框架下，即使使用结构良好的prox函数（如负熵），加速仍是不可能的。这与ℓ1范数光滑性下的加速可行性形成鲜明对比，揭示了相对光滑假设本身的结构性限制。同时，量子设置中的推广结果对量子优化算法的理论分析具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: We prove an $Ω(L/T)$ lower bound for the convergence rate of minimization in the class of functions that are convex and $L$-smooth relative to negative entropy on the standard $d$-simplex, valid for every first-order method when $d = Ω(T^2)$. In particular, this shows that mirror descent is optimal up to a logarithmic factor in this class. This may be surprising due to the fact that accelerated methods are readily available under the assumption of smoothness in $\ell_1$-norm. While Dragomir et al. (Mathematical Programming, 2022) have already showed that acceleration might be impossible under relative smoothness, their prox-function is pathological and constructed together with the hard instance. In contrast, we show non-acceleration for a specific prox-function with particularly favorable structure. We also extend the result to the quantum setting, proving the same lower bound in the class of functions $L$-smooth relative to negative von Neumann entropy on the spectrahedron of $d \times d$ Hermitian positive-semidefinite matrices with unit trace.

</details>


### [223] [Branching out: Prognostics-Based Replacement Policies for Series Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27899)
*Daniel Koutas,Daniel Straub*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合规划方法，用于推导基于故障预测的预测性维护策略。该方法整合了决策选项、故障预测模型提供的系统未来状态信息以及更新-奖励过程的成本，最终得到仅由少数参数定义的策略，可通过理论分析或从运行至故障数据优化确定。在预防性更换和预防性订购两个决策场景中验证了方法有效性，数值结果表明该策略性能与优化基准策略相当，但效率更高且对过拟合更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性维护需要有效的决策策略，但现有方法往往面临策略参数过多、计算复杂或容易过拟合等问题，难以在实际场景中高效部署。

Method: 提出一种混合规划方法，将决策选项、故障预测模型的未来状态信息以及更新-奖励过程的成本纳入统一框架，通过理论推导或数据优化确定少数关键策略参数。

Result: 在预防性更换和预防性订购两个预测性维护决策场景中，所提策略性能与优化后的基准策略相当，同时在计算效率和抗过拟合能力方面具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 该混合规划方法能够在保证维护策略性能的同时大幅简化策略表示，提高决策效率与鲁棒性，为实际预测性维护应用提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid planning method for deriving prognostics-based predictive maintenance policies. The method accounts for the available decision options, the information on the future state of the system provided by a prognostic model, and the costs of the underlying renewal-reward process. It results in policies defined by only a few parameters, which can be determined based on theoretical considerations or by optimization from run-to-failure data. We demonstrate the potential of the method in two separate predictive maintenance decision settings: preventive replacement and preventive ordering. Numerical investigations show that the derived policies rival the performance of optimized benchmark policies, while being significantly more efficient and robust against overfitting.

</details>


### [224] [The Existence and Stability of Generalized Multi-Source Weber Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27900)
*V. S. T. Long,N. M. Nam,T. H. Tai,C. Tammer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在最小时间函数框架下研究具有集值目标的一般化多源Weber问题，建立了全局和局部最优解的存在性，分析了解集的定性性质（闭性、紧性、有界性/无界性），推导了目标函数和最优值函数关于目标集扰动的Lipschitz连续性，并引入解映射研究了其稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源Weber问题是一类重要的设施选址优化问题，传统研究多假设目标为点集。本文将其推广到集值目标情形，并将问题置于最小时间函数的统一框架下，以提供更一般的数学处理工具，同时弥补该问题在灵敏度分析方面的理论空白。

Method: 在最小时间函数框架下建模一般化多源Weber问题；利用变分分析和集值分析工具建立最优解的存在性定理；考察解集的闭性、紧性及有界性/无界性条件；通过扰动分析推导目标函数和最优值函数的显式Lipschitz连续性；基于集值分析引入全局和局部解映射并研究其稳定性（如上半连续性、下半连续性等）。

Result: 获得了全局和局部最优解的存在性结果；刻画了解集的定性性质（闭性、紧性、有界性条件）；给出了目标函数和最优值函数关于目标集扰动的显式Lipschitz常数估计；建立了全局与局部解映射的稳定性性质，从而提供了该问题定量与定性的灵敏度分析结果。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了集值目标多源Weber问题在解集性质与灵敏度分析方面的理论空缺，为更广泛的选址优化问题提供了系统的数学框架和分析工具，具有潜在的应用价值。

Abstract: This paper studies the generalized multi-source Weber problem with set-valued targets in the framework of minimal time functions. We first establish the existence of global and local optimal solutions and investigate several qualitative properties of the corresponding solution sets, including closedness, compactness, and conditions ensuring boundedness or unboundedness. Next, we derive explicit Lipschitz continuity properties for the objective function and the associated optimal value function with respect to perturbations of the target sets. We then introduce the global and local solution mappings and study their stability properties from the viewpoint of set-valued analysis. These results provide a quantitative and qualitative sensitivity analysis for the generalized multi-source Weber problem in the setting of minimal time functions.

</details>


### [225] [OptGraph: Large Language Models Enhanced Evolutionary Optimization Via Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27918)
*Xianchao Xiu,Jianhao Li,Huangyue Chen,Wanquan Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: OptGraph是首个引入图检索增强生成（GraphRAG）的优化智能体工作流，通过构建可复用的类型化经验图谱，利用图邻域信息增强知识检索，支持自适应知识更新，在基准数据集上平均精确准确率比最优提示式自动化优化框架高8.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的自动化进化优化方法存在三个局限：模式复用能力不足、缺乏错误感知的精细化改进、跨多样化任务的检索鲁棒性差。作者旨在解决这些问题，构建更强大的自动化优化框架。

Method: OptGraph采用图检索增强生成（GraphRAG）技术：1）将可复用经验构建为类型化图，捕获建模模式、问题形式化、实现细节和错误修正之间的关系；2）在推理阶段利用图邻域信息丰富检索知识，提供结构化上下文以改进建模、验证和迭代细化；3）支持自适应知识更新，将执行轨迹和验证反馈蒸馏为可复用的图知识，无需进行LLM参数微调。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，OptGraph相比最先进的基于提示的自动化优化框架，平均精确准确率提升了8.9%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: OptGraph通过GraphRAG有效弥补了现有LLM自动化优化方法在模式复用、错误感知精细化以及检索鲁棒性方面的不足，并证明了基于图谱的知识管理与自适应更新机制是提升优化性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a powerful tool for automated evolutionary optimization, but existing methods remain limited in pattern reuse, error-aware refinement, and retrieval robustness across diverse tasks. To address these limitations, we propose OptGraph, the first optimization agentic workflow that introduces graph retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG). Specifically, OptGraph first constructs reusable experience as a typed graph, capturing the relationships among modeling patterns, problem formalization, implementation details, and error corrections. In the inference stage, OptGraph leverages graph neighborhood information to enrich retrieved knowledge, providing structured context to improve modeling, verification, and iterative refinement. Moreover, OptGraph supports adaptive knowledge updates, enabling the distillation of execution traces and verification feedback into reusable graph knowledge without ndertaking LLM parameter tuning. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets show that our proposed OptGraph achieves an average exact accuracy 8.9% higher than the state-of-the-art prompt-based automated optimization frameworks. Our code has been made available at https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/OptGraph.

</details>


### [226] [Global exponential turnpike properties for optimal control of the viscous Burgers equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28042)
*Emmanuel Trélat,Xingwu Zeng,Can Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一维粘性Burgers方程在局部内部控制下的二次最优跟踪问题，建立了全局指数turnpike性质。对于充分小的周期跟踪目标，任意初始数据的有限时域最优解都趋近于唯一最优周期状态；零目标情形则在原点处实现全局稳态turnpike，且对初始数据无小性假设。这是粘性Burgers方程首个全局指数turnpike结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Burgers方程是最具代表性的非线性抛物型偏微分方程之一，其最优控制问题的turnpike性质（即有限时域最优解在大部分时间内接近稳态/周期最优解）在工程与经济应用中具有重要价值。已有关于Burgers方程的turnpike结果多为局部性质或依赖小初值假设，建立全局的指数turnpike性质是该领域尚未解决的核心问题。

Method: 证明方法分为两部分：(1) 通过严格凸性和周期Riccati理论建立局部指数turnpike性质；(2) 利用抛物型耗散论证构造一个与时间区间长度无关的"吸收时间"（absorbing time），使得任意初始数据在该时间内被吸收到局部turnpike吸引域内，从而将局部结果推广为全局结果。

Result: (1) 对充分小的周期跟踪目标，证明了全局指数turnpike性质：任意初始数据的有限时域最优解指数逼近唯一的最优周期状态；(2) 对零跟踪目标情形，无需对初始数据施加小性假设即可获得原点处的全局稳态turnpike。这是粘性Burgers方程首批全局指数turnpike结果。

Conclusion: 通过将局部指数turnpike（基于严格凸性与Riccati理论）与抛物耗散带来的均匀吸收时间相结合，成功克服了非线性带来的全局性困难，为一般非线性抛物方程的高维推广和更广泛的控制结构下建立全局turnpike性质提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: We establish global exponential turnpike properties for quadratic optimal tracking problems governed by the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation with localized internal control. For every initial datum, finite-horizon optimal solutions approach the unique optimal periodic regime when the periodic tracking target is sufficiently small; the zero-target case yields a global steady turnpike at the origin, with no smallness assumption on the initial datum. To our knowledge, these are the first global exponential turnpike results for the viscous Burgers equation. The proof combines a local exponential turnpike, obtained through strict convexity and periodic Riccati theory, with a parabolic dissipation argument that provides an absorbing time independent of the horizon.

</details>


### [227] [Multi-Asset Utility Maximization with Jump Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28051)
*Sigui Brice Dro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在含跳跃信号的多维风险资产环境中，投资组合效用最大化问题。采用鞅最优性原理推导出带跳跃的倒向随机微分方程（BSDE）来刻画价值函数和最优策略，证明了BSDE解的存在唯一性，并讨论了个体异质跳跃信号情形，最后对对数效用情形下的高斯信号进行了数值分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将一维指数效用情形下的方法推广到多维布朗运动与独立泊松随机测度驱动的风险资产场景，并考虑投资者可接收跳跃信号（包括异质信号）的情况，填补该设定下组合效用最大化的理论空白。

Method: 首先将投资组合动态表示为半鞅过程；其次利用鞅最优性原理推导带跳跃的BSDE，以其解来刻画价值函数和最优策略；随后证明该BSDE解的存在唯一性；最后对对数效用情形下高斯信号进行数值模拟。

Result: 成功推导了多维情形下带跳跃的BSDE表征框架，证明了相关BSDE解的存在唯一性，并通过高斯信号的数值示例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为多维风险资产、含跳跃信号（含异质信号）的投资组合效用最大化问题提供了统一的BSDE理论框架及数值验证。

Abstract: In this paper, we study portfolio utility maximization problem in a setting where the risky asset is driven by a multidimensional Brownian motion and an independent homogeneous Poisson random measure, and where strategies may incorporate jump signals. Following the same approach as in the one-dimensional case for the exponential utility function [17], we first represent the portfolio dynamics as semimartingale processes. We then use martingale optimality principle to derive the corresponding backward stochastic differential equation (BSDE) with jumps to characterize both value function and an optimal strategy in terms of its solution. We subsequently prove the existence and uniqueness of the solution to the related BSDE with jumps. We also address the idiosyncratic case i.e where the investor receive heterogeneous jump signals. Finally, we provide numerical illustrations with Gaussian signals for the logarithmic case.

</details>


### [228] [On Leader Selection for Strong Structural Controllability in Matrix-Weighted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28168)
*Lanhao Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文解决了矩阵加权网络中最小领导者集选择以实现强结构可控性（SSC）的NP-hard逆合成问题，提出了严格的数学框架，将结构不可控归因于维度特定的可达性隔离和拓扑对称等价，并设计了两个阶段的合成方法及三种对称破缺算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵加权网络中的最小领导者集选择问题以确保强结构可控性是一个尚未解决的NP-hard挑战，需要一个严格的数学框架来系统性地解决这一难题。

Method: 首先证明结构不可控完全源于两类瓶颈：维度特定的可达性隔离和拓扑对称等价。在此基础上提出两阶段合成框架：第一阶段为可达性先决条件，用于识别结构根节点；第二阶段包含三种对称破缺算法——贪婪Weisfeiler-Lehman选择、子模界最大化、以及划分熵最大化。

Result: 通过数学证明确保了对不变子空间和结构扩张的免疫性，并在多种拓扑结构上进行了广泛的数值评估以验证方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的框架为矩阵加权网络的强结构可控性问题提供了严格的理论保证和实用的求解方案，通过对称破缺和可达性分析有效克服了NP-hard挑战。

Abstract: The inverse synthesis problem of selecting a minimal leader set to guarantee strong structural controllability (SSC) in matrix-weighted networks remains an unresolved NP-hard challenge. This paper proposes a rigorous mathematical framework to solve this. We prove that structural uncontrollability stems exclusively from dimension-specific reachability isolation and topological symmetry equivalence. To overcome these bottlenecks, we formulate a two-phase synthesis: a reachability prerequisite to identify structural roots, followed by three distinct symmetry-breaking algorithms (Greedy Weisfeiler-Lehman Selection, Submodular Bound Maximization, and Partition Entropy Maximization). Mathematical proofs guarantee immunity to invariant subspaces and structural dilation, validated by extensive numerical evaluations across diverse topologies.

</details>


### [229] [The optimality of an (s, S) hiring policy on a workforce planning problem with fixed recruitment costs and binomial turnover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28171)
*Zhen Chen,Roberto Rossi,Belen Martin-Barragan,S. Armagan Tarim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时域人力资源规划问题，其中每期员工流失服从二项分布，参数取决于招聘后的劳动力水平。模型包含固定招聘成本，目标是最小化包括招聘、工资和短缺成本在内的期望总成本。通过引入二项K-凸性概念，证明了最优招聘策略具有(s,S)型结构，并提出分段近似方法转化为混合整数线性规划求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人力资源规划中员工流失具有随机性和决策依赖性，传统模型未充分考虑招聘行为对流失分布的影响，且固定招聘成本的存在使得问题难以直接求解。需要建立能刻画决策依赖转移概率的随机动态规划模型，并为最优策略提供理论保证和高效求解方法。

Method: (1) 建立有限时域随机动态规划模型，其中员工流失服从参数依赖招聘后劳动力水平的二项分布，并纳入固定招聘成本；(2) 证明变量单期成本的离散凸性和期望总成本的K-凸性；(3) 提出二项-K-凸性（Binomial-K-convexity）概念，证明K-凸性在Bellman函数二项传播下得以保持；(4) 证明最优招聘策略为(s,S)型结构；(5) 开发分段近似方法，将问题转化为混合整数线性规划（MILP）以求解每期的(s,S)参数。

Result: 理论结果表明最优招聘策略具有(s,S)结构：当劳动力水平低于阈值s时将员工招聘至S水平，否则不招聘。数值实验显示所提出的MILP方法计算速度快且最优性间隙小。

Conclusion: 该研究为决策依赖转移概率下的人力资源规划问题提供了完整的理论框架（K-凸性保持证明）和高效的实用求解算法，为具有固定招聘成本的企业招聘决策提供了理论依据和计算工具。

Abstract: We study a finite-horizon workforce planning problem in which staff turnover in each period follows a binomial distribution whose parameters depend on the post-hiring workforce level. The model incorporates a fixed hiring cost that is incurred whenever recruitment occurs, regardless of the number of employees hired. The objective is to minimise the expected total cost, including recruitment, salary, and shortage costs, where deviations below period-specific staffing requirements are penalised. To analyse this stochastic dynamic programme with decision-dependent transition probabilities, we establish the discrete convexity of the variable single-period cost (the sum of expected salary and penalty costs) and the K-convexity of the expected total cost. Specifically, we introduce the concept of Binomial-K-convexity to facilitate the proof that K-convexity is preserved under Binomial propagation in the Bellman function. We then show that the optimal hiring policy exhibits an (s, S)-type structure: when the workforce level in a given period falls below a threshold s, staff are hired up to level S; otherwise, no hiring occurs. Furthermore, we develop a piecewise approximation approach that yields a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) formulation for solving the problem and computing the (s, S) parameters for each period. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves fast computation with small optimality gaps.

</details>


### [230] [No-gap second-order conditions for optimization problems involving transport distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28264)
*Nicolas Borchard,Christian Meyer,Gerd Wachsmuth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了度量空间中的优化问题，以到给定先验测度的传输距离作为正则项。利用弱-*二阶次微分理论推导了无间隙型的二阶最优性条件，并在光滑目标函数和Kantorovich势的额外假设下，建立了与二次增长的等价性。同时计算了弱-*二阶次微分并证明了弱-*上微分可微性，最后将结果应用于度量空间中的最优控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在度量空间（测度空间）中求解带有传输距离正则项的优化问题，需要建立严格的二阶最优性条件。传统的二阶条件理论在测度空间中面临挑战，需要引入弱-*拓扑下的次微分理论来刻画最优性条件，特别是希望获得无间隙类型的二阶必要条件与充分条件之间的等价关系。

Method: 本文采用弱-*二阶次微分理论来推导二阶最优性条件。首先利用Kantorovich对偶问题的解（即Kantorovich势）来分析传输距离正则项的二阶结构；其次计算弱-*二阶次微分并证明弱-*上微分（epi）可微性；最后在光滑目标函数和Kantorovich势的适当假设下，建立二阶最优性条件与二次增长之间的等价性。

Result: 成功推导了无间隙型的二阶最优性条件，证明了弱-*二阶次微分的存在性和弱-*上微分可微性，并建立了在额外假设下二阶条件与二次增长的等价关系。所获得的二阶理论适用于度量空间中的最优控制问题。

Conclusion: 本文为度量空间中带传输距离正则项的优化问题提供了完整的二阶变分分析框架，填补了该领域二阶最优性条件的理论空白，为后续应用于最优控制等问题奠定了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We consider optimization problems in the space of measures. As a regularization term, the problem includes the transport distance to a given prior measure. For the derivation of second-order optimality conditions of no-gap type, the theory of weak-$\star$ second subderivatives is used which will lead to an equivalence with quadratic growth under additional assumptions on the smooth part of the objective and on the Kantorovich potential, i.e., the solution of the dual transport problem. Further, the weak-$\star$ second subderivative is calculated and weak-$\star$ epidifferentiability is proven. Finally, the results are applied to optimal control problems in measure space.

</details>


### [231] [Exact and Approximate Solvability of Systems Involving Proximity Operators in Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28343)
*Mark Allien D. Roble*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究实Hilbert空间中有限族正常下半连续凸函数的邻近算子方程组的存在性与唯一性，建立了近似解存在的充要条件，并引入逆邻近性质（IPP）作为逆最佳逼近性质（IBAP）的推广，最后应用于可行性问题和信号恢复问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的投影问题可被视为邻近算子方程的特例，将投影问题推广到更一般的邻近算子系统具有理论意义和应用价值。作者希望通过系统研究此类方程组，建立解的性态刻画，并推广已有的IBAP概念。

Method: 利用凸分析和非线性分析的工具，对形如 pr(f_i)(x)=p_i 的邻近算子方程组进行研究：（1）建立近似解存在性的充要条件；（2）引入逆邻近性质（IPP）并给出多种等价刻画；（3）将IPP与已有的IBAP进行对比和推广。

Result: 给出了邻近算子方程组近似解存在性的充要条件，建立了IPP的多种等价刻画，并证明IPP是IBAP的自然推广。此外，在可行性问题和信号恢复问题中展示了所得结果的应用。

Conclusion: 本文为Hilbert空间中邻近算子方程组提供了完整的理论框架，IPP的引入拓展了经典IBAP的研究视野，所得结果对优化和逆问题领域具有潜在的应用价值。

Abstract: Let $\HH$ be a real Hilbert space and let $(f_i)_{i\in I}$ be a finite family of proper, lower semicontinuous, and convex functions on $\HH$. This study investigates the existence and uniqueness of exact solutions to systems involving proximity operators of the form: \
  $(\forall i\in I)\ \pr{f_i}(x)=p_i,$
  where $(p_i)_{i\in I}$ is a prescribed collection of proximal points in $\HH$, which naturally generalize classical projection problems. We establish necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of approximate solutions to such systems. Moreover, we introduce and derive several characterizations of the inverse proximal property (IPP), as a generalization of the inverse best approximation property (IBAP). Applications of the obtained results are presented in the contexts of a feasibility problem and signal recovery problem, demonstrating the relevance of the proposed framework to optimization and inverse problems in Hilbert spaces.

</details>


### [232] [Beyond Hand-Derived Inequalities: Decision Diagrams for Cut Generation in Binary Polynomial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28511)
*Martin Cooper,Margarita Castro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于决策图（DD）的方法用于二元多项式优化（BPO）的割平面生成。对于任意选定的支撑结构，该方法在局部多线性多面体中分离出定义面的割，并将其提升回原问题。文章还利用新奇的紧凑DD编码获得了多线性多面体的扩展公式、基于有效反链的宽度刻画（发现了一类新的多项式可解超图），以及生成割的面性证书。实验表明该方法在根节点实现了更大的间隙闭合、使用更少的割，并显著加速了分支定界过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，BPO的强不等式（如two-links、flowers、odd β-cycles）是针对固定支撑模式手工推导的，缺乏一般性和系统性。作者希望提出一种通用方法，能够为任意支撑结构自动生成定义面的强割平面，从而克服手工推导的局限性。

Method: 提出决策图（DD）方法：(1) 基于递归公式构建紧凑的DD编码，仅表示顶点变量，超边变量隐式编码在状态表示中；(2) 从DD编码出发，对任意选定的支撑在对局部多线性多面体中分离出定义面的割，并将其提升回原问题；(3) 探索三种支撑策略（复用、扩展、分区为局部超图的截面结构）用于割生成；(4) 该DD编码还衍生出扩展公式、宽度刻画（通过有效反链发现新的多项式可解超图类）以及割的面性证书。

Result: 实验结果显示，基于DD的方法在根节点实现了比现有方法更大的间隙闭合，且使用更少的割，同时显著加速了分支定界过程。

Conclusion: 决策图方法为BPO割平面生成提供了一个通用、自动化的框架，克服了传统手工推导支撑模式特化不等式的局限性，并在理论和实验两方面都展现了显著的优越性。

Abstract: We study cutting-plane generation for binary polynomial optimization (BPO), whose feasible region is the multilinear set of a hypergraph. Strong inequalities for this set---such as two-links, flowers, and odd $β$-cycles---are classically hand-derived for fixed support patterns. Instead, we propose a decision-diagram (DD) approach: for any chosen support, it separates a facet-defining cut in the local multilinear polytope and lifts it back to the original problem. We utilize a novel compact DD encoding based on a recursive formulation that represents only the vertex variables and implicitly encodes the hyperedge variables inside the state representation. From this DD encoding, we also obtain: (i) an extended formulation of the multilinear polytope, (ii) a width characterization via valid antichains that uncovers a new polynomially solvable class of hypergraphs, and (iii) a certificate for the facetness of the generated cuts. We explore three different support strategies for cut generation that reuse, expand, or partition local structures into section hypergraphs. Our empirical results show that our DD-based methodologies achieve a larger gap closure at the root node with fewer cuts than existing procedures and, in turn, markedly accelerate branch-and-bound procedures.

</details>
